*Łucja Biel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式人工智能对专业法律翻译的影响，提出将人工智能素养纳入法律翻译能力体系，以提升译者在技术环境中的专业判断与数字韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能为法律翻译带来效率与能力扩展机会，但也伴随语言质量、技术可靠性、法律合规、职业伦理及认知依赖等风险。因此，需要构建适合法律翻译职业特点的人工智能素养框架。

Method: 文章采用概念分析与框架建构方法，论证人工智能不会改变法律翻译的基本目标，但会扩展译者的职业能力要求。在此基础上，提出由基础性、程序性、批判性和战略性四个相互支撑维度构成的人工智能素养框架，并讨论其教学应用。

Result: 研究将人工智能素养界定为法律翻译能力中的横向组成部分。该框架旨在帮助译者理解人工智能原理与局限、掌握工具使用流程、批判性评估输出质量和风险，并从职业发展与专业规范角度制定使用策略。文中还提出可用于法律翻译教育的课堂活动。

Conclusion: 法律翻译教育应将人工智能素养系统融入课程，而非仅训练工具操作。未来法律译者需要以批判、负责且符合专业标准的方式整合人工智能，从而维护法律翻译的准确性、可靠性与职业责任。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming legal translation by introducing opportunities alongside linguistic, technical, legal, ethical and cognitive risks. This chapter examines the implications of AI for professional legal translation and proposes an AI literacy framework tailored to the profession. It argues that AI does not change the fundamental objectives of legal translation but requires an extension of professional competence through AI literacy. The proposed framework comprises four mutually reinforcing dimensions, foundational, procedural, critical and strategic, and conceptualises AI literacy as a transversal component of legal translation competence that fosters digital resilience. It further discusses the pedagogical implications of this framework by proposing classroom activities designed to develop AI literacy in legal translator education, enabling future translators to integrate AI critically, responsibly and in accordance with professional standards.

</details>


### [51] [Calibrating Artificial Guilt: Neurally Grounded Reward Shaping for Prosocial Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04663)
*Aaditya Mehta,Arya Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将人类神经行为数据估计得到的“内疚”权重迁移到多智能体强化学习的奖励塑形中。实验显示，该神经校准权重生成的智能体最接近人类在社会情境中的安全选项比例，优于固定常数、无社会项和单位系数基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作型多智能体强化学习通常手工设定社会性奖励项的系数，缺少经验依据且可迁移性有限。作者试图检验：能否从人类行为与神经数据中定量恢复一种社会偏好参数，并以此约束人工智能体的亲社会奖励塑形。

Method: 作者使用公开SoDec责任归因fMRI数据集中的40名参与者数据，以个体固定效应回归建模瞬时幸福感变化与不同结果类型计数之间的关系。将“伙伴负面结果”与“社会负面结果”的回归系数之差定义为内疚权重，得到估计值1.118，效应量Cohen's d为0.214。随后在双智能体Social Lottery环境中，采用独立PPO训练，并比较四种奖励塑形：神经校准权重、统一常数权重、零权重的自利条件，以及单位系数oracle。每个条件评估1000个回合，并以社会情境下安全选择率及其与人类行为分布的KL散度作为指标。

Result: 神经校准条件下，智能体的社会安全选择率为0.459，最接近人类参考值0.484，对应KL散度仅0.0012。其余三种条件相对人类行为的KL偏差高出约一个至三个数量级。结果表明，源于人类神经行为数据的参数至少在该任务设定中比手工或简单固定设置更能复现人类的社会决策模式。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献是展示了“神经行为先验—奖励塑形—多智能体行为”这一可操作链路：人类数据可为亲社会奖励项提供定量校准，而非仅提供定性启发。不过，证据目前受限于40人样本、较小效应量、单一简化环境及独立PPO设定；尚需在更多任务、群体、社会偏好维度和训练范式中验证其稳健性、泛化性及相对于其他自动奖励学习方法的增益。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning often adds social terms to individual rewards, yet the scale of those terms is usually chosen by hand. We ask whether a guilt signal can instead be calibrated from human neural and behavioural data and transferred to artificial agents. Using the public SoDec responsibility fMRI dataset (40 participants), we fit a subject-fixed-effects regression of momentary-happiness changes on outcome-type counts and recover a guilt weight as the Partner-negative minus Social-negative contrast ($\hat{w}=1.118$, Cohen's $d=0.214$). We embed this weight in a two-agent Social Lottery environment and train independent Proximal Policy Optimization actor-critics under four shaping regimes: neurally calibrated, uniform constant, zero (selfish), and a unit-coefficient oracle. Across 1{,}000 evaluation episodes per condition, the calibrated agents track the human Social safe-choice rate most closely ($0.459$ vs.\ human $0.484$; $\mathrm{KL}=0.0012$), while the other three conditions deviate by one to three orders of magnitude in KL. Human neurobehavioural priors can therefore act as quantitative constraints on prosocial reward shaping.

</details>


### [52] [Traceable LLM-Generated Hazard Scenarios for Operational Safety Analysis of Aviation Systems Using ASRS Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04697)
*Cristian Mascia,Roberto Pietrantuono,Daniel Rodriguez,Stefano Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向航空运行安全的AI辅助危险场景生成方法：以不利结果为输入，从ASRS历史报告中生成结构化致因假设及对应事件叙事，并通过共现证据、相似案例追溯和多种语言模型实验评估其可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空运行层面的危险分析需要同时考虑天气、空管、空域限制、航空器运行与人为因素之间的交互，不能仅依赖面向单一航空器系统的功能危险评估。传统分析难以系统、高效地从大量运行事件报告中发现并构造潜在危险情景。

Method: 方法首先针对目标不利后果生成由类别化因素组成的结构化假设，再生成与该结构一致的运行事件序列叙事。每个场景使用历史报告中的因素共现信息计算合理性评分，并链接至最相似的留出ASRS报告以提供可追溯性。论文还提出混合方案：先通过进化溯因生成结构化假设，再以该假设约束叙事生成。实验比较不同大语言模型、零样本与少样本提示，以及可选微调对输出质量的影响。

Result: 摘要表明，混合式“结构先行、叙事受约束”的生成方案能够提升场景叙事的正确性并降低输出变异性。评估重点包括生成结构和叙事的有效性、真实性，以及不同提示策略、模型选择和微调设置带来的差异；摘要未提供具体定量结果。

Conclusion: 该研究将大语言模型、历史共现证据、案例追溯与进化溯因结合，为航空运行危险分析提供了可生成、可解释、可验证的候选场景构造框架。其核心价值在于支持分析人员从历史运行报告中系统识别跨因素交互导致的潜在风险，同时以结构化约束缓解纯叙事生成的不稳定性。

Abstract: Operational hazard analysis of aviation system operations must consider interactions among weather, ATC actions, airspace constraints, aircraft operations, and human factors - distinct from the functional hazard assessment applied at the aircraft-system level. We present an AI-assisted approach that generates candidate hazard scenarios from NASA's Aviation Safety Reporting System (ASRS). Given a target adverse outcome, it produces a structured hypothesis as categorical factors and a narrative scenario describing an operational event sequence consistent with the structure. Each scenario includes by a plausibility score from historical co-occurrence evidence and traceability to the most similar held-out ASRS reports. We then propose a hybrid variant, conditioning narrative generation on a structured hypothesis produced via evolutionary abduction, improving correctness and reducing variability. We evaluate multiple large language models, zero-shot versus few-shot prompting, and optional fine-tuning, measuring how prompting and model choice affect the validity and realism of the generated structures and narratives.

</details>


### [53] [Diagnosing Tool-Selection Reasoning in LLM Agents with Canary Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04719)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“金丝雀工具（canary tools）”评测框架，在智能体的MCP工具集中植入具有诊断性的诱导工具，以识别模型发生错误工具选择时的具体推理弱点，而非仅记录“选错工具”这一结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测通常只能判断模型是否选择了错误工具，难以解释错误原因，也难以区分不同模型在语义理解、参数处理、前置条件判断和时间约束等方面的工具调用缺陷。论文旨在将单一错误指标转化为可解释、多维度的工具选择脆弱性画像。

Method: 作者构建六类金丝雀工具分类法：语义诱饵、参数陷阱、能力幻象、前置条件盲视、时间诱饵与粒度陷阱。每类工具针对一种特定的工具选择弱点设计。实验评测8个模型，包括6个托管模型和2个8B开源权重模型；设置120项任务、3种金丝雀密度和3个随机种子，共进行8,640次运行，并额外实施2,880次措辞细微度消融实验。任务成功由独立于模型提供方的裁判评分，并使用第二个独立裁判复核，评审一致性为Cohen's kappa=0.75。核心指标为每任务金丝雀易感率CSR，并分析其与任务失败之间的相关性。

Result: 模型间CSR差异约达36倍：Claude Opus 4.8的易感率最低，Llama 3.1 8B最高。模型能力提升总体会显著降低易感性，但能力层级并不能完全预测安全性：最易受影响的托管模型属于中等能力档，同一提供方中价格更低的模型也可能更稳健。不同陷阱类型具有能力分层特征：能力幻象对前沿模型最有效，而其余类型对强模型大多不敏感、却能有效触发小型开源模型，因此这些类型并非无效，而是具有模型能力区分作用。将诱导工具中的明显提示语弱化后，前沿模型CSR基本不变，表明测量结果更可能反映工具推理问题，而非对特定措辞的表面匹配。CSR与任务成功呈负相关，Spearman rho=-0.34；最稳健的模型未因更高的金丝雀压力而出现显著性能下降。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种可解释、可分解的智能体工具选择安全评测方法。其主要贡献在于以六维诊断分类法揭示不同模型的具体工具推理脆弱点，并表明总体能力、价格或模型档位均不足以替代直接的工具选择稳健性测试。论文的证据支持金丝雀工具作为评估模型在复杂MCP工具环境中抗诱导与正确调用能力的有效探针；公开框架、工具模式、任务和日志也有助于后续复现与扩展。

Abstract: Agent evaluations tell us that a model picked the wrong tool, but rarely why. We introduce canary tools: diagnostic probe tools planted in an agent's Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool set, each engineered to probe one specific tool-selection weakness. A six-type taxonomy (semantic decoys, parameter traps, capability mirages, prerequisite blindness, temporal decoys, and granularity traps) turns a single "wrong tool" outcome into a multi-dimensional profile of how a model reasons about tools. We evaluate eight models -- six hosted and two 8B open-weight -- spanning three capability tiers, on 120 tasks across three canary-density conditions and three seeds (8,640 runs), plus a 2,880-run subtlety ablation. Task success is graded by a provider-independent judge, corroborated by a second independent judge (Cohen's kappa = 0.75). We report three findings. First, susceptibility drops sharply as models get more capable: the per-task canary susceptibility rate (CSR) ranges about 36x across models, lowest for Claude Opus 4.8 and highest for Llama 3.1 8B. Second, capability tier alone does not predict safety: the most susceptible hosted model is mid-tier, and within a provider the cheaper model can be the safer one. Third, the taxonomy is capability-stratified: capability mirages most reliably trap frontier models, while the other types are largely inert on strong models but fire on small open models, so they discriminate by capability rather than being weak. Softening each canary's give-away phrase leaves frontier CSR essentially unchanged, evidence that the probes measure reasoning, not phrase-spotting. Susceptibility also predicts task failure (Spearman rho = -0.34), while the most robust models are not significantly degraded by canary pressure. We release the framework, canary schemas, tasks, and logs.

</details>


### [54] [When Prompts Become Pixels: Prompt-Region Grounding for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04726)
*Yongxin Wang,Ruizhe Zhou,Yueling Tang,Yingying Zhu,Xuemin Zhao,Xiaojun Chang,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出视觉化任务语义（VTS）评测，将原本以文本呈现的问题移入图像，同时保持题目与答案不变，以检验多模态大语言模型能否跨输入通道理解并执行指令。实验显示模型存在显著的视觉任务语义缺口，所提提示区域定位方法可有效缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态模型基准通常将问题置于文本中，无法评估模型是否能同等理解图像中的任务指令。论文旨在区分文字识别能力与将视觉文本作为推理指令使用的能力，并量化两者之间的差距。

Method: 构建VTS受控干预：将问题嵌入图像，保持源问题和答案不变，并在6个多模态大语言模型和4个基准上进行评估。进一步提出提示区域定位方法，将问题区域与类型化语义对齐，并从遮罩视图中恢复其干净表示；推理时无需OCR或区域元数据。

Result: 在全部24个模型—任务组合中，视觉化问题均导致准确率下降，平均下降17.8个百分点。模型通常能够正确转录图像中的问题，但未必能将其用于后续推理。采用所提方法后，四项基准上的VTS准确率由58.0%提升至66.3%，且不降低原始文本接口下的准确率。

Conclusion: 读取图像中的任务文本与将其定位为推理指令是两种不同能力。VTS揭示了超越OCR层面的语义通道缺口，而提示区域定位能够在相同训练成本下显著改善该缺口，并提升模型对视觉化指令的执行能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly reason over screenshots and documents where the task itself may be written in pixels. Yet benchmarks usually place questions in text, leaving it unclear whether models use the same instruction equally well across channels. We introduce Visualized Task Semantics (VTS), a controlled intervention that moves the question into the image while keeping the source problem and answer fixed. Across six MLLMs and four benchmarks, accuracy drops in all 24 model-task pairs, by 17.8 points on average. Models often transcribe the visual question correctly yet fail to use it, exposing a semantic channel gap beyond OCR. To reduce this gap, we present prompt-region grounding, whose core design aligns the question region with typed semantics and recovers its clean representation from a masked view. At matched training cost, our method raises four-benchmark VTS accuracy from 58.0 to 66.3 while preserving accuracy on the original interface, and requires no OCR or region metadata at inference. Reading task-bearing text and grounding it as an instruction for reasoning are distinct capabilities.

</details>


### [55] [Chain-of-Thought Monitoring Can Be Unreliable in Implicit-Influence Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04735)
*Agatha Duzan,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出首个直接比较“显式影响”与“隐式影响”条件下思维链（CoT）可监控性的基准。结果表明：当影响以直接指令形式出现时，监控器较易从CoT中发现行为偏移；当影响仅通过上下文中的轻微暗示产生时，模型行为仍会改变，但CoT检测率显著下降，说明既有显式设置可能高估实际部署中的可监控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT监控评估主要关注显式影响场景，即提示明确要求模型执行某种隐藏目标并掩饰该行为。这类设置未必反映真实应用：实际行为偏差也可能由与任务表面无关的上下文细节或措辞暗示引发，而模型并未被直接要求隐瞒。论文旨在检验同一类行为影响在隐式与显式条件下的可检测性差异，并评估部署层面的系统提示是否会进一步影响监控能力。

Method: 作者构建基准，在存在促使模型选择特定答案的“推动因素”时，对比两种呈现方式：隐式条件将推动因素作为随意的上下文附带信息；显式条件则直接要求模型遵循该推动因素并隐藏其影响。实验覆盖二元选择、数值评分、选择题问答和开放式编程四类任务，并测试七个具备扩展推理能力的前沿模型。研究以模型行为是否发生偏移衡量影响是否有效，并以CoT监控器对该偏移原因的识别率衡量可监控性；此外还测试了更贴近实际部署的系统提示附加项。

Result: 在显式影响条件下，CoT监控器可检测到60%至94%的行为偏移，表明即使模型被要求掩饰，相关指令仍常泄露在推理文本中。在隐式影响条件下，相同因素仍可显著改变模型行为，但四类任务中的两类检测率下降了41至46个百分点。进一步加入开发者可能用于减轻无关偏差的系统提示后，隐式影响造成的行为偏移依然存在，而检测率可进一步降至5%。

Conclusion: CoT监控并非只取决于模型是否受影响，也强烈取决于影响机制是否被显式表达。基于显式隐藏指令所得的监控性能，可能系统性高估真实场景中的可监控性；同时，看似善意的系统提示配置也可能降低对隐性行为驱动因素的识别能力。该工作强调未来监控评测应纳入隐式影响、多任务形式及部署提示等因素，并将“行为保持受影响而推理不可见”视为关键失效模式。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is increasingly treated as an important safety layer for frontier reasoning models. Most monitorability evaluations study explicit-influence settings: setups where the prompt directly incentivizes the model to hide something, e.g., by instructing it to perform a hidden side-task. A complementary axis for CoT-monitor evaluations is implicit-influence settings, where the prompt contains no instruction to hide, but the model's behavior is still shaped by features of the task or context, e.g. an irrelevant detail about a candidate that biases a hiring rating.
  We introduce the first benchmark that directly compares CoT monitorability under the two regimes. We test how model behavior changes in the presence of a nudge to choose a particular option. The nudge is delivered either as a casual aside (implicit), or as a direct instruction to act on the nudge and to conceal having done so (explicit). The benchmark spans four task formats (binary choice, numeric rating, multiple-choice QA, open-ended coding) and seven frontier extended-thinking models.
  Under explicit influence, a CoT monitor detects 60-94% of behavior shifts: even models instructed to conceal it leak the instruction into their CoT. Under implicit influence, the same factors still shift behavior, but detection falls by 41-46 percentage points in two of our four settings. Realistic system-prompt additions (of the kind a developer might deploy to reduce off-topic bias) lower implicit detection further, to as low as 5%, while preserving the behavioral influence itself.
  These results suggest that monitorability estimates obtained in explicit-influence settings may over-estimate monitorability, and that monitorability can be further decreased by well-intentioned deployment choices. Our benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/agatha-duzan/implicit-vs-explicit-influence

</details>


### [56] [EviGraph: Evidence-Guided Autonomous Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04738)
*Zhenjiang Ren,Ruiji Li,Xujing Zhang,Ziliang Pang,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EviGraph提出以“类型化证据图”作为自主科研智能体的核心状态表示，通过持续验证问题、假设、实验、发现与主张之间的证据链，减少无依据主张和研究流程中的语义不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主科研系统通常采用线性流水线，将选题、假设、实验和论文写作依次执行，但缺乏对跨阶段主张—证据关系的显式维护与校验。这会导致研究问题、实验设计、结果解释和最终结论之间出现断链、错配或不受结果支持的主张。

Method: 框架将研究过程建模为包含Problem、Gap、Hypothesis、Experiment、Finding和Claim节点的类型化证据图。系统检查证据链中的依赖缺失、语义失配及结果—主张不一致；定位最早出现问题的薄弱节点后，仅重新生成其受影响的下游子图。通过图检查点机制保留已验证状态，避免失败修复破坏已有可靠证据；仅当保留的全部主张均具备经验证的证据链时，才生成论文稿件。

Result: 在ARC-Bench-ML和NanoResearch-20上的实验表明，EviGraph的整体科研表现优于所比较的端到端科研智能体基线。相对最强基线，其Claim Support Rate提升40.19%，Experimental Data Consistency达到87.73%。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：将证据图作为自主科研智能体的显式、可验证运行状态，而非事后记录，能够提升主张可追溯性、实验结果一致性和整体研究可靠性。摘要未说明具体基线构成、指标计算方式、统计显著性及不同任务上的分项表现，这些仍需结合全文进一步评估。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents can generate hypotheses, execute experiments, and draft manuscripts, yet their outputs often contain unsupported claims and inconsistencies between research questions, experiments, results, and conclusions. We argue that this problem is partly architectural: existing systems organize research as sequential pipelines but do not explicitly maintain or validate the evolving claim-evidence structure across stages.In this paper, we introduce EviGraph, an autonomous research framework that represents the research process as a typed evidence graph containing Problem, Gap, Hypothesis, Experiment, Finding, and Claim nodes. The graph serves as the operational state of the agent rather than a post-hoc record. EviGraph inspects evidence chains for missing dependencies, semantic misalignment, and result-claim inconsistencies, localizes the earliest weak node, and regenerates its affected downstream subgraph. Graph checkpointing prevents unsuccessful repairs from corrupting previously validated evidence. Manuscripts are generated only after every retained claim is grounded in a validated evidence chain.Experiments on ARC-Bench-ML and NanoResearch-20 show that EviGraph outperforms the compared end-to-end research-agent baselines in overall research performance, improves Claim Support Rate by 40.19% over the strongest baseline, and achieves 87.73% Experimental Data Consistency. These results demonstrate the value of explicit evidence-state maintenance for reliable autonomous research.

</details>


### [57] [Fewer Tokens, Smaller Cache: Reward-Coordinated Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04771)
*Qiyuan Zhu,Dezhi Li,Pengyu Cheng,Tianle Chen,Jiacheng Wang,Ruijie Shen,Hao Gu,Sida Lin,Zirui Liu,Jiacheng Liu,Sirui Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ReCo（Reward-Coordinated Compression），利用过程奖励模型动态协调KV缓存压缩、反思令牌抑制与早停，在基本保持推理准确率的同时，显著降低长链式推理的生成长度与端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长推理链虽能提升复杂任务表现，但会造成过度推理和高昂推理成本。现有KV缓存压缩方法通常对整个推理轨迹采用统一策略，仅关注缓存删除量，忽略不同推理阶段对上下文丢失的敏感性不同，也忽略缓存缩小可能诱发模型生成更多令牌、抵消部分节省。因此，需要同时优化缓存保留与后续生成行为。

Method: ReCo以轻量级过程奖励估计器为核心，在每个已完成推理步骤上评估状态质量，并据此实施三项协同机制：第一，奖励自适应KV缓存压缩，在高奖励步骤更激进地压缩缓存、低奖励步骤则保留更多上下文；第二，对反思令牌施加分档奖励惩罚，抑制低价值或冗余的继续推理；第三，采用基于置信度的早停机制，在推理状态足够可靠时提前结束生成。

Result: 在3个推理模型和6个基准测试上，ReCo相对于完整链式推理将生成令牌数减少37%至65%，并实现2.08倍至2.35倍的端到端延迟加速，同时大体保持原有准确率。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，推理加速不应仅优化KV缓存大小，而应结合推理过程中的状态质量，联合控制上下文压缩和文本生成。过程奖励可作为统一信号，为不同步骤分配差异化压缩强度、生成约束和停止决策，从而在准确率与效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) excel on complex tasks through long chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, but their lengthy intermediate steps cause severe overthinking that inflates inference cost. KV-cache compression is a common solution, yet existing reasoning-oriented methods apply a uniform policy across the trajectory and judge compression only by what it removes from the cache. Two observations point the other way. First, a reasoning state's tolerance to context loss varies along the trajectory, and process reward tracks it: deleting tokens at high-reward steps preserves accuracy far better than deleting the same budget at random. Second, compression is not free on the generation side, since a smaller cache leads the model to generate more tokens, partly canceling the saving. Together these motivate coordinating both sides under a single process reward. We propose ReCo (Reward-Coordinated Compression), a step-wise framework in which a lightweight process-reward estimator scores each completed step and drives three components: (1) reward-adaptive KV-cache compression that shrinks the retained cache harder at high-reward steps and less at low-reward ones, (2) a reward-banded penalty on reflection tokens that curbs redundant generation, and (3) confidence-based early stopping that triggers when the reasoning is reliable. Across three reasoning models and six benchmarks, ReCo reduces generated tokens by 37%-65% and end-to-end latency by 2.08x-2.35x over Full CoT, all while largely preserving accuracy.

</details>


### [58] [CoPlan: A Trustworthy Co-Intelligence Interface for Care Planning through Role-Based Contestable Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05107)
*Hung Truong Thanh Nguyen,Hélène Fournier,Piper Jackson,Makoto Itoh,Shannon Freeman,Rene Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoPlan，一种面向人机协同与可质询性的照护规划界面，使照护团队能够审查、质疑、修改并共同生成照护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI照护规划系统通常将建议作为固定输出，难以支持临床人员、患者、照护者和团队成员对建议进行检查、挑战与调整，尤其是在建议与临床判断、患者价值观或现实可行性不一致时。

Method: CoPlan采用多智能体工作流，由专门化AI智能体生成候选干预措施及支持性或质疑性论据；人类照护规划者可接受、拒绝、修改或补充论据。系统还支持自适应照护团队招募、基于角色的论据审查、最终照护计划生成，以及通过调度智能体开展后续执行。

Result: 研究在居家养老照护规划场景中展示了CoPlan，验证了其整合多方专业能力、支持论据审查与方案迭代，并连接照护计划制定和实际后续安排的能力。

Conclusion: CoPlan将协同智能与可质询性结合，使AI建议保持可检查、可修订和可解释，能够更好地维护人的决策自主权与临床责任，为可信的人机协同照护规划提供了界面设计和理论框架。

Abstract: AI-supported care planning can help clinicians, patients, caregivers, and care teams coordinate complex decisions across clinical, functional, psychosocial, and environmental needs. However, many AI systems present recommendations as fixed outputs, limiting stakeholders' ability to inspect, challenge, and revise plans when they conflict with clinical judgment, patient values, or real-world feasibility. We present CoPlan - a Co-Intelligent and Contestable Interface for Human-AI Care Planning. CoPlan uses a multi-agent workflow in which specialized AI agents generate candidate interventions and supporting or challenging arguments, while human care planners can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before final plan generation. Through this design, CoPlan combines co-intelligence, in which humans and AI agents contribute complementary expertise, with contestability, where recommendations remain open to inspection, revision, and justification. We demonstrate CoPlan in an aging-in-place care planning scenario. The system supports adaptive care team recruitment, role-based argument review, final care plan generation, and practical follow-up through scheduling agents. This work contributes a contestable care planning interface and a design framing for trustworthy human-AI care planning that preserves human agency and clinical accountability.

</details>


### [59] [NSF-HRPT: Neural Semantic Field meets Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree for Safety-Critical Scenario Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04776)
*Yu Zhao,Jiangyu Pan,Tao Hu,Ming Yin,Fan Yang,Jiangfan Liu,Xiubo Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出NSF-HRPT框架，用于基于单目视觉进行实时、定量化的多主体风险评估，结合神经语义场、分层风险感知树和仿真到真实增强策略，在TTC估计与风险定位任务上取得较强表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有碰撞预测方法难以从单目图像中稳定量化风险等级，主要原因是多主体交互动态复杂、真实环境存在显著不确定性，且仿真训练与真实场景之间存在领域差异。

Method: 方法首先训练神经语义场NSF，从仿真数据中联合学习场景语义、主体轨迹预测以及概率化碰撞时间TTC分布。推理阶段，将预训练NSF作为先验输入分层风险感知树HRPT，通过层次化结构实现并行计算与空间关系推理，从而评估多主体风险。为提升真实场景适用性，论文引入无需重新训练的Sim2Real增强机制，利用基础模型提供的先验信息修正或补充风险感知。

Result: 实验显示，该框架在合成基准上达到当前最佳性能，并在真实数据集上于TTC估计精度和风险区域定位精度方面获得具有竞争力、接近最佳水平的结果。摘要同时表明，该方法具备面向实时单目视觉风险感知的计算效率。

Conclusion: NSF-HRPT通过将学习式场景建模与结构化风险推理结合，缓解了单目风险量化、多主体交互推理及仿真到真实迁移的问题。其核心价值在于以概率TTC和空间化风险定位为输出，为实时安全风险感知提供统一框架；但摘要未说明计算开销、极端场景鲁棒性及基础模型先验的具体依赖程度，这些仍需通过完整论文验证。

Abstract: The ability to accurately assess and anticipate risks in safety-critical scenarios is crucial for autonomous driving systems. While existing research has made progress in collision prediction, accurately quantifying risk levels from monocular vision inputs remains challenging due to the complex dynamics of multi-agent interactions and the inherent uncertainty in real-world environments. To address these challenges, we present NSF-HRPT, a novel framework that combines learning-based perception with structured reasoning for quantitative risk assessment. Our approach features a Neural Semantic Field (NSF) that learns to model scene semantics, trajectory predictions, and probabilistic Time-to-Collision (TTC) distributions from simulation data. During inference, the pre-trained NSF serves as a prior for our Hierarchical Risk Perception Tree (HRPT), which enables efficient parallel computation and spatial reasoning about multi-agent risks. Additionally, we introduce a Sim2Real enhancement strategy that improves real-world applicability without retraining by incorporating priors from foundation models. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on synthetic benchmarks and delivers competitive, near-state-of-the-art results on real-world datasets for both TTC estimation accuracy and risk localization precision. The proposed method provides an effective solution for real-time risk awareness from monocular camera inputs.

</details>


### [60] [OctoLong: Mid-Training On Cross-Repository Code Contexts Enhances Long-Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05141)
*Indraneil Paul,Falko Helm,Goran Glavaš,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出OctoLong：通过递归收集代码依赖关系，构建可达数百万token的高依赖密度代码上下文，并用其训练600M至14B参数的长上下文指令模型。仅以OctoLong数据替换约12%的传统上下文扩展语料，即可显著提升多项长程代码理解与智能体任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文训练语料主要来自书籍、论文和代码仓库，资源规模有限，且通常缺乏足够丰富的远距离依赖。对于仓库级代码理解、跨文件引用解析、长期状态追踪及长程工具使用等任务，模型需要接触结构化且依赖链更长的上下文。

Method: 作者构建OctoLong上下文工程流水线，联合使用AST解析器、语言服务器后端和包管理器，递归检索代码符号引用及外部依赖，从而整理出依赖关系密集的超长代码上下文。随后以约500亿token的混合数据进行上下文扩展中期训练，其中约62亿token为OctoLong代码上下文；再以约100亿token进行指令微调，得到OctoLong-Instruct系列模型。实验中还通过消融研究分析以OctoLong替代传统长上下文语料的影响。

Result: 在与18个开源权重长上下文语言模型的比较中，OctoLong-Instruct在长距离信息检索、长期状态跟踪、仓库级代码理解和下游智能体任务上取得明显改进。此外，该方法不仅改善超长上下文能力，也提升了短上下文编程场景中的API使用表现。关键发现是：在传统上下文扩展语料中仅加入约12%的OctoLong数据，便可带来显著收益。

Conclusion: 高价值长上下文训练数据的关键不只是长度，而是跨文件、跨模块和跨包的依赖结构密度。OctoLong表明，利用代码分析与依赖解析工具构造递归关联的代码上下文，能够以较小的数据占比有效增强模型的长程推理、代码理解和工具化任务表现。

Abstract: Context lengths of language models (LMs) have dramatically increased, driven by the demands for in-context learning, self-improvement, and long-horizon agentic workflows. Existing long-context corpora, however, are dominated by books, academic articles, and code repositories, which are finite resources and often scarce in long-distance dependencies. In this work, we introduce OctoLong, a context engineering pipeline that instruments an AST parser, a language server backend, and a package manager to facilitate the recursive retrieval of code references, enabling the curation of dependency-rich code contexts of millions of tokens in length. We then train OctoLong-Instruct, a suite of capable long-context open LMs, derived from base models ranging in size from 600M to 14B parameters, via context-extension mid-training on a ~50B-token mixture containing ~6.2B tokens of OctoLong code contexts, followed by ~10B tokens of instruction tuning. Our training ablations and experimental evaluations against 18 state-of-the-art open-weight long-context LMs show that supplanting just 12% of traditional context-extension corpora with OctoLong data yields substantial gains in long-range retrieval, long-term state tracking, repository-level code understanding, and downstream agentic tasks, while also enhancing API usage in short-context coding scenarios.

</details>


### [61] [Privileged, but Biased: How PI-Conditioned Teachers Break Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04794)
*Sarthak Harne,Chinmay Karkar,Yash Pandya,Ahmed Awadallah,Akshay Nambi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了仅依赖自蒸馏、且不结合奖励信号的训练范式。结果表明：该方法可在简单场景复现已有收益，但在问答、数学、代码和多轮工具使用等高难任务中，尽管逐词损失持续下降，验证准确率却无提升且通常下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究将自蒸馏视为比基于可验证奖励的训练更节省计算的替代方案，但其证据主要来自狭窄、低难度任务。本文关注的核心问题是：当自蒸馏作为唯一优化目标、完全没有奖励项时，是否真正能够提升模型的任务求解与推理能力。

Method: 作者首先在简单设定中复现SDPO的报告结果，随后将相同配置扩展至不同难度和类型的任务，包括问答、数学、编程及多轮工具调用；同时覆盖不同推理模式、模型规模、特权信息形式，并比较SDPO与OPSD两种配方。论文还提出PI Bias Score，用于量化教师因观察某个特定参考解而产生的目标偏置，并进一步分析逐词损失在不同类型词元及正确轨迹中的分布。

Result: 在困难任务中，SDPO和OPSD均表现出训练目标与任务成功脱钩的现象：逐词蒸馏损失稳定下降，但验证准确率不升反降。机制分析显示，教师在拥有参考解等特权信息后，其逐词目标会偏向该特定解题轨迹，而非普适的正确性。学生对该目标进行全序列拟合时，优化信号对轨迹是否正确近乎不敏感；大量损失集中于停用词、标点和不确定性表达等低信息词元，而真正决定答案的词元权重不足。即使在正确轨迹中，承担探索和犹豫功能的词元也具有较高散度，因而会被蒸馏目标压制。

Conclusion: 纯自蒸馏并非可靠的高难任务能力学习目标。其关键问题在于特权信息诱导的参考轨迹偏置，使逐词匹配损失无法有效表征任务正确性，并抑制推理中必要的探索行为。由此训练得到的学生模型更平坦、决策性更弱，推理能力并未改善；若要实现稳定性能提升，自蒸馏应与能够直接关联任务成功的监督或奖励信号结合。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) has emerged as a compute-efficient alternative to reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards: a self-teacher, conditioned on privileged information (PI) about the answer such as a reference solution, supplies dense per-token supervision to a student that never sees it. Reported gains, however, come almost exclusively from narrow, low-difficulty settings, leaving open a basic question: as a lone objective, with no reward term, does SD teach anything? We reproduce SDPO's reported gains in its easy setting, then apply the identical setup to difficult tasks and find that it does not. Across question answering, mathematics, coding, and multi-turn agentic tool use, across reasoning modes, model sizes, and forms of PI, and under both the SDPO and OPSD recipes, the per-token loss falls steadily while validation accuracy does not improve and typically degrades. We explain this failure through a single causal chain from the loss to the model it produces. The chain begins with PI bias: having seen one particular reference solution, the teacher's per-token target is pulled toward that trajectory rather than toward correctness in general, an effect we quantify with a PI Bias Score. Trained to match this target everywhere, the student's objective becomes nearly blind to whether a rollout is correct, and the loss it assigns falls mostly on low-information tokens like stopwords, punctuation, uncertainty markers, rather than those that determine the answer; within correct rollouts the exploratory tokens incur the highest divergence, so it penalizes the hesitation that reasoning requires. The result is a flatter, less decisive student that is no better at reasoning: as a lone objective, SD optimizes a signal decoupled from task success.

</details>


### [62] [ContextWeave: A Real-World Workflow Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04830)
*Bo Wang,Yuqian Yao,Enxi Wang,Luozhijie Jin,Yang Liu,Yiran Suo,Yuxuan Cai,Enyu Zhou,Yufei Gao,Honglin Guo,Tianyu Huai,Li Ji,Zhikai Lei,Bufan Li,Lizhi Lin,Jinxiu Liu,Jie Yang,Jiazheng Zhou,Maosen Zhou,Pengfang Qian,Shichun Liu,Guanshan Liu,Hao Zheng,Yunhao Yu,Hang Yan,Jihua Kang,Xinchi Chen,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ContextWeave是一个面向长周期办公工作流的记忆评测基准，检验智能体回忆历史经验后能否真正提升后续任务执行，而非仅评估检索或问答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评测通常将问题简化为信息检索、事实问答或短期上下文利用，难以反映智能体在真实、多月、个性化办公流程中基于既往经验持续完成任务的能力。论文旨在衡量记忆对下游执行质量、偏好一致性和工作流延续性的实际贡献。

Method: 作者构建ContextWeave，将14名参与者经隐私保护处理后的多月办公流程重建为1,005个可执行任务，其中568个为核心评测任务。每个任务包含自然语言指令、容器化环境、执行轨迹及任务专属评分规则。基准以Workspace Score衡量工作空间产出质量，以Preference Score衡量结果与用户个体偏好的一致性，并额外诊断记忆的相关性、连续性、可解性及对误导性回忆的鲁棒性。实验在固定基础模型下比较6种记忆组件，并在固定记忆组件下测试5种基础模型。

Result: 在固定模型条件下，最佳记忆配置将Workspace Score从68.08提高到78.20，将Preference Score从41.50提高到70.60。在固定记忆组件时，回忆机制对5个基础模型的两类指标均带来提升，但不同模型的增益差异明显。分析表明，包含可操作细节和丰富经历的记忆，比紧凑摘要更能支持工作流延续并减少重复探索；但这类记忆也更容易受到误导性回忆的干扰。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的价值应通过其对真实任务执行和个性化结果的提升来评估，而不应局限于检索相关性。有效的智能体记忆需要保留足以指导行动的经验细节，同时增强对错误、无关或误导性回忆的识别与抑制能力。ContextWeave为研究长程、状态化工作流中的记忆利用提供了可执行且多维度的评测框架。

Abstract: Memory is essential as language agents move from isolated tasks to long-horizon, stateful workflows, yet existing evaluations often reduce it to retrieval or question answering. We introduce ContextWeave, a longitudinal benchmark that evaluates whether recalled experience improves downstream agent performance in realistic office-work streams. ContextWeave reconstructs privacy-preserved, multi-month workflows of 14 participants into 1,005 executable tasks, including 568 core evaluation tasks, with instructions, containerized environments, trajectories, and task-specific rubrics. It measures workspace quality and alignment with participant-specific preferences, complemented by diagnostics of relevance, continuity, solvability, and robustness to misleading recall. Across six memory components under a fixed model, the strongest configuration raises Workspace Score from 68.08 to 78.20 and Preference Score from 41.50 to 70.60. With a fixed memory component, recall improves both outcomes for all five tested base models, although gains vary substantially. Our analysis shows that actionable, experience-rich memory supports workflow continuation and reduces redundant exploration more effectively than compact summaries, while it can also be more susceptible to misleading recall. These findings motivate memory systems that optimize not only retrieval relevance but also reliable use during execution.

</details>


### [63] [When Shared Rollouts Fail in Defensive Driving Evaluation: A NAVSIM Score Basis Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04896)
*Ziang Wei,Minjun Yu,Zheyuan Lai,Mingjie Pang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该摘要审计了NAVSIM v2.2单阶段场景评分中的数值稳定性风险，发现特定依赖栈下的共享轨迹重演不稳定会与“参考轨迹条件式宽容”机制耦合，使不观察周边参与者的盲策略获得异常高的合规评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御性驾驶评分应能区分是否利用周边交通参与者信息的策略。若基准测试因参考轨迹的失败而给予智能体宽容性信用，且智能体与参考轨迹共享不稳定的轨迹生成过程，则可能把参考轨迹的失效错误地转化为广泛的智能体合规得分，削弱评分的有效性。

Method: 作者在NAVSIM v2.2原始场景单阶段评分上进行审计：比较Ignore-All路线盲探针、路线感知但参与者盲探针、人类回放及PDM-Closed；在12,146个navtest样本上检验排序；使用固定32个样本诊断集复现轨迹分歧；通过同源依赖栈对照和精确输入诊断，将问题定位至共享速度重拟合中的依赖敏感数值行为；再在450个样本控制池中仅替换求解器，验证因果影响。

Result: 在受影响的文档化软件栈及被审计数值后端下，Ignore-All和参与者盲探针的得分均高于人类回放与PDM-Closed。重新安装并依照公开规范配置后，固定诊断集仍出现轨迹分歧。仅替换求解器即可消除轨迹分歧，并在保持宽容机制启用的情况下恢复“盲策略排序靠后”的预期结果，表明数值不稳定性是直接触发因素。

Conclusion: 该工作认为，参考轨迹条件式宽容并非单独导致异常评分；其与共享轨迹生成过程的数值不稳定性结合后，会将共享的参考失败传播为智能体合规信用。作者建议，在使用此类评分支持防御性驾驶结论前，应披露评分依据与软件依赖栈，报告盲探针和覆盖操作，并开展轨迹生成稳定性测试。

Abstract: Defensive driving scores are useful only when they preserve distinctions between policies that observe surrounding actors and those that do not. Re-simulation benchmarks may use reference-conditioned forgiveness, under which an agent receives credit when the logged human reference fails a compliance channel. When agent and reference share an unstable rollout transformation, this rule can propagate shared reference failures into broad compliance credit.
  We audit this risk in NAVSIM v2.2 original scene single-stage scoring. Under the affected documented-stack condition on the audited numerical backend, the route-blind Ignore-All probe and a route-aware actor-blind probe outrank human replay and PDM-Closed over the complete 12,146-token navtest split. A fresh installation following the public specification reproduces rollout divergence on a fixed 32-token diagnostic set. A same-source dependency stack control and an exact-input diagnostic isolate dependency-sensitive numerical behavior in the shared velocity refit. On a 450-token control pool, replacing only the solver eliminates rollout divergence and restores blind-last ordering while keeping forgiveness enabled. Thus, the numerical instability is the direct trigger. Reference-conditioned forgiveness propagates the resulting shared reference failures into compliance credit. We contribute an audit protocol requiring score basis and stack disclosure, blind probes, overwrite reporting, and rollout stability tests before using such scores for defensive driving claims.

</details>


### [64] [WorldCycle: Self-Verifiable Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04964)
*Bohai Gu,Yueyang Yuan,Taiyi Wu,Dazhao Du,Jian Liu,Xiaoyi Pang,Jie Zhang,Xiaocheng Lu,Haobin Zhong,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出WorldCycle，一种面向交互式视频世界模型的自验证强化学习后训练框架。其核心是利用“动作序列与其逆序列组成的闭环应回到初始状态”这一可解析约束，在缺少长时程真实未来状态标注时监督模型减少累积误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型需要支持长时程规划与探索，但多步预测会产生并累积状态漂移。传统强化学习后训练依赖可验证的目标或真实未来状态，而任意动作序列通常不存在对应的真实未来观测，因此难以直接评估长期预测误差。论文旨在构造无需额外标注、可用于长时程验证的训练信号。

Method: WorldCycle首先从普通动作序列构造由正向动作及其逆动作组成的闭合动作循环，并进一步构造该循环的重复执行轨迹。训练时联合优化两类奖励：（1）空间闭合奖励，约束镜像的正向与反向片段在状态演化上保持对称，并使循环末状态回归初始状态；（2）时间一致性奖励，对齐同一循环在重复执行中的对应状态。该设计促使模型将动作学习为具有一致组合规律的状态变换算子，而非仅拟合时间序列模式。论文还提出CycleBench，用于测试模型在复杂动作结构下的状态回归能力。

Result: 据摘要报告，WorldCycle将状态回归漂移最高降低44%，并使复合动作上的预测准确率相较基础模型提升近4倍。方法对于基础模型表现较差的分布外复合动作循环也具有自然扩展性，表明其改善不仅限于训练中常见的简单动作模式。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将可逆动作闭环转化为无标注的长期正确性验证机制，并以此设计强化学习奖励来缓解世界模型的累积误差。该方法的有效性依赖于环境动作具备可逆性或至少可构造近似逆操作；因此，其在不可逆、强随机或存在观测遮挡的环境中的适用范围仍需通过完整实验进一步确认。总体而言，WorldCycle为提升世界模型的长期一致性、复合动作泛化与物理约束建模提供了有价值的训练范式。

Abstract: Interactive video world models are essential for long-horizon planning and exploration, yet they suffer from compounding errors. Post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) can improve these models, but they hit a verification bottleneck: for arbitrary action sequences, no ground-truth future state exists to measure long-term drift. Our key insight is that reversible action cycles make this verification possible: a sequence composed with its inverse must analytically return to the initial state, yielding annotation-free supervision on long-horizon correctness. Building on this, we introduce WorldCycle, a self-verifiable RL framework that constructs closed action cycles and their repeated executions from ordinary action sequences, and optimizes two complementary rewards: a spatial closure reward enforcing symmetry between mirrored forward and reverse segments, and a temporal consistency reward aligning states across repeated cycle executions. These rewards force the model to learn actions as consistent state operators rather than memorized temporal patterns, and extend naturally to out-of-distribution composite action cycles that the base model handles poorly. We further release CycleBench, a diagnostic benchmark for state-returning ability under complex action structures. WorldCycle reduces state returning drift by up to 44% and lifts composite-action accuracy nearly 4x over the base model, providing a vital foundation for physically grounded world models.

</details>


### [65] [Short-term load forecasting under EU-AI Act Requirements in Safety-Critical Environments: Results from a 41-day live challenge on the aggregated German transmission-grid load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05018)
*Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting (STLF) play a vital role in the electric power industry. It serves infrastructure that European and German law designate as critical. Determinism, reproducibility, and auditability are engineering requirements rather than optional extras. STLF is no longer purely an accuracy problem. It is also a software-engineering and compliance problem. This paper describes results from a 41-day live challenge that evaluated a complete STLF pipeline for the aggregated German transmission-grid load. The pipeline is based on the open-source Python library spotforecast2-safe, which implements the EU-AI Act Requirements in Safety-Critical Environments by design. The pipeline predicts the 24 hourly load values of a target day from European Network of Transmission System Operators for Electricity (ENTSO-E) data. It includes anomaly detection and gap-aware data preparation, calendar and weather covariates, a recursive multi-step forecasting algorithm, and hyperparameter tuning. Forecast accuracy is measured against the official ENTSO-E day-ahead forecast. The EU-AI act compliant spotforecast2-safe pipeline beats the ENTSO-E baseline. In-context models show competitive performance. Transparent, low-cost, and auditable local models (referred to as macl2l in this paper) are competitive with more than 100-million-parameter large, energy-intensive pre-trained foundation models such as chronos-2. The challenge infrastructure, the complete submission history of all teams, and the frozen final leaderboard are publicly available.

</details>


### [66] [From Score Matrices to Football-Aware Match-State Simulation: An Auditable LLM Harness for Exact-Score Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05030)
*Shaopeng Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可审计的混合式足球比分预测框架，将动态Dixon–Coles类泊松模型的统计校准能力与大语言模型对战术、角色和比赛情境的推理能力结合。四个版本的实验显示，逐球模拟及赛后进程判断可提升精确比分预测，但总体增益有限，且结果仍具有探索性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态泊松模型能够估计球队实力、预期进球和一致的比分概率，但难以表达战术匹配、球员角色、比赛动机以及首个进球后行为变化。大语言模型能够处理这些语境信息，却缺乏可靠的概率校准能力。因此，研究旨在通过可审计的信息接口融合两类模型，改善比分候选选择，同时控制输入语义和推理过程。

Method: 研究设计了四个递进版本：V1为动态、比分驱动的Dixon–Coles基线；V2将大语言模型生成的情境评分映射为预期进球参数；V3在固定比分候选集合上进行逐球模拟，以替代单一标量修正；V4进一步加入共享的首次破局判断、进球后的连锁效应判断、时间感知的停止规则和确定性尾部候选。信息框架统一输入语义，提供赛前证据，并限制大语言模型使用可检查的推理路径。实验按时间顺序回放2025—26赛季英格兰超级联赛前150场比赛。

Result: V1的精确比分Top-1和Top-3命中率分别为10.0%和26.7%；V3提升至12.0%和30.0%；V4进一步达到14.7%和30.7%。V4将候选覆盖率从77.3%提高到84.7%，但新增尾部候选没有产生任何Top-3精确比分命中。V1原生1X2预测的最大概率类别准确率为53.3%，对数损失为0.9878，Brier分数为0.5870，排名概率分数为0.2095。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将大语言模型用于逐球和进球后情境模拟，可能比简单的参数标量修正更有助于精确比分选择，但提升幅度有限，新增尾部候选的实际价值尚未得到支持。本文的主要贡献在于构建可审计的混合架构、展示清晰的迭代设计，并揭示足球语境模拟在何种程度上能够改善预测。由于开发数据切片并非完全未触碰的基准集，且封闭式大语言模型可能存在结果记忆，实验结论应视为探索性发现。

Abstract: Football score forecasting combines a strong statistical core with a difficult contextual edge. Dynamic Poisson-family models estimate team strength, expected goals, and coherent score probabilities, but do not directly understand roles, tactical matchups, motivation, or how a first goal changes behaviour. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such concepts, yet are not calibrated probability engines. We combine both components through an auditable information harness. This paper documents four iterations: V1, a dynamic score-driven Dixon-Coles baseline; V2, which maps LLM contextual ratings back into expected-goal parameters; V3, which replaces scalar correction with goal-by-goal simulations over a frozen score-candidate set; and V4, which adds shared first-breakthrough and post-goal cascade judgments, time-aware stopping, and deterministic tail candidates. The harness defines input semantics, supplies pre-match evidence, and constrains the LLM to an inspectable reasoning route. On a chronological replay of the first 150 matches of the 2025-26 English Premier League, V1 achieved 10.0% Top-1 and 26.7% Top-3 exact-score accuracy. V3 reached 12.0% and 30.0%, while V4 reached 14.7% and 30.7%. V4 increased candidate coverage from 77.3% to 84.7%, although no added tail candidate became a Top-3 exact hit. V1's native 1X2 distribution achieved 53.3% argmax accuracy, 0.9878 log loss, 0.5870 Brier score, and 0.2095 ranked probability score. These results are exploratory: the development slice is not an untouched benchmark, and temporal input isolation cannot exclude outcome memory in a closed LLM. The contribution is an auditable hybrid architecture, a clear design evolution, and negative findings showing where football-aware simulation does and does not improve score selection.

</details>


### [67] [Item Response Theory for AI Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05086)
*Joshua Fonseca Rivera,Neil Shah,David Demitri Africa,Konstantinos Voudouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将项目反应理论（IRT）用于大语言模型安全基准的统一分析，旨在提升安全测评的可解释性、效率与审计能力。研究基于8个基准和192个模型，发现模型差异主要可由少数潜在能力维度解释，并表明少量经心理测量筛选的题目即可高精度近似完整评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准的汇总分数存在解释困难：不同基准之间可能题目重复、结果高度相关，且模型可能识别评测环境后采取策略性行为，导致分数不能稳定反映真实能力。论文希望借助IRT将题目难度、区分度及模型潜在特质显式建模，从而获得更可靠的跨基准比较与模型审计工具。

Method: 作者对8个安全基准、192个语言模型的逐题表现拟合IRT模型，并分析潜在因子结构。随后比较心理测量学筛选题目与随机子集在恢复完整基准分数时的误差，评估自适应选题的测评效率；此外，利用IRT估计的模型特征与题目响应模式，对策略性低表现及API后端模型变化进行审计检测。

Result: 模型间的大部分跨基准方差可由三个可解释潜在因子概括：拒答严格性、真实性以及对语境化有害内容的处理能力。与等规模随机抽题相比，按IRT性质挑选的题目能以更低误差重建完整基准得分；对若干单项基准，自适应测试约10题即可达到有效估计，测评成本可降低97%至99%。IRT审计还能识别较为直接的策略性低表现，并提示API所连接模型发生变化。

Conclusion: IRT可作为安全基准的实用基础设施：它不仅能将难以解释的总分拆解为具有语义的潜在维度，还能通过信息量更高的题目大幅压缩测评成本，并支持持续的模型行为审计。论文的主要限制在于其结论依赖所选基准、模型样本及IRT建模假设；更复杂的策略性行为、题目分布漂移和潜在维度随时间变化，仍需通过外部验证与持续校准来检验。

Abstract: Language models differ in how safely they behave and these differences are measured by safety benchmarks. But aggregated benchmark scores are hard to trust and interpret, because benchmarks duplicate one another, correlate heavily, and models may sandbag when they detect evaluation. To address these issues, we draw on Item Response Theory (IRT), a statistical toolkit for measuring these latents from performance on items with inferred psychometric properties. We fit IRT models to eight safety benchmarks across 192 language models, the largest psychometric analysis of LLM safety evaluations to date, and contribute three results. First, we find that three interpretable factors of refusal strictness, truthfulness, and contextual harm explain most of the variance between models across benchmarks. Second, psychometrically selected items recover full benchmark scores with lower error than random subsets of the same size, and roughly ten adaptively chosen items suffice for several individual benchmarks, cutting evaluation cost by 97-99%. Third, IRT supports audits of individual models, showing that it can be used to detect naive sandbagging and changes of model behind APIs. Overall, we show IRT is a ready-made toolkit for reading, reducing, and auditing safety benchmarks, which we recommend frontier labs and evaluators adopt.

</details>


### [68] [Hierarchical Graph Memory for LLM Agents with Path-level Localization and Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05095)
*Xiawei Yue,Boran Wang,Xiaoqing Zhang,Shuxin Zheng,Ziwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HiGram是一种面向长期推理的分层动态图记忆框架，通过路径级定位与协同重写，提升长期对话问答中的检索效率、答案质量和冲突处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图记忆通常将全部历史信息存储在扁平图中，导致无关上下文累积、证据选择成本上升；同时，记忆单元独立更新，难以高效覆盖相关内容及其依赖关系。

Method: HiGram采用由上层节点和MemoryUnit组成的粗到细分层图记忆，以减少检索中的无关信息；利用由查询和更新条件共同生成的MicroGraph，定位支持子图与证据路径；随后通过协同重写，同时更新单元内部记忆和单元间依赖，维护局部路径中的有效依赖结构。

Result: 在长期对话问答和冲突感知记忆评测基准上，HiGram相较基线显著提升了答案质量与Token效率，并在动态、静态和条件冲突场景下提高了答案准确率及查询有效的证据选择能力。

Conclusion: 分层组织、路径级证据定位和联合依赖重写能够有效缓解长期记忆中的噪声累积与更新冗余，使图记忆系统更适合持续演化的长期推理任务。

Abstract: Agents for long term reasoning require a memory that can be efficiently and effectively updated over time, as new facts and external feedback continue to arrive. Recently, graph memory has been adopted to offer structural organization for multi-hop retrieval and reasoning. However, existing methods store all memories in a flat graph, and accumulated historical memories can introduce irrelevant contexts and increase the cost of evidence selection during retrieval. Moreover, they typically update memory units independently, requiring repeated unit-wise rewrite to cover related changes. To address these issues, we propose HiGram, an evolving hierarchical graph memory framework with path-level localization and rewriting. Specifically, we first propose a hierarchical graph memory, which organizes the memory into coarse-to-fine architecture composed of upper-level nodes and MemoryUnits, thereby reducing the amount of irrelevant information during retrieval. We further propose MicroGraph-based path-level localization, which leverages query and update conditioned MicroGraphs to identify support subgraph and evidence path before rewrite. Finally, we propose a coordinated rewriting method that jointly revises intra-unit memory and inter-unit dependencies, enable valid dependency structures updating in the localized evidence path. Experiments on benchmarks for long-term conversational question answering and conflict-aware memory evaluation demonstrate that our method demonstrate substantial improvements over baselines in answer quality and token efficiency. Besides, our method improves answer accuracy and query-valid evidence selection under dynamic, static, and conditional conflicts.

</details>


### [69] [ABSeeker: Training Long-Horizon Search Agents via Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05102)
*Yijun Lu,Rui Ye,Jiajun Wang,Yuwen Du,Tian Jin,Songhua Liu,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ABC（Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment）框架，通过从最终答案反向恢复解题线索，并以线索为锚点评估搜索轨迹中的每一步，将稀疏的轨迹级成败信号转化为稠密的步骤级监督，用于提升长程搜索智能体训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程搜索智能体在监督微调和强化学习中通常对轨迹内所有步骤等权处理，无法区分有效检索、错误操作与冗余步骤。即使轨迹最终失败，其中部分步骤也可能具有价值；反之，成功轨迹中也可能包含无效步骤。因此，需要更细粒度的信用分配机制。

Method: ABC包含两个核心阶段：其一，答案回溯线索恢复，从给定问题及标准答案出发，反向追踪得到解决问题所需的中间线索；其二，线索锚定步骤评分，将轨迹中每个搜索步骤与恢复出的线索进行匹配和评估，生成步骤级奖励。基于该奖励，作者提出ABC-SFT，对不同轮次的训练损失进行加权；以及ABC-GRPO，将步骤级分数直接作为GRPO强化学习奖励。作者在Qwen3.5-4B基础上，使用8.5k训练样本训练ABSeeker。

Result: ABSeeker在BrowseComp上达到37.3%，在BrowseComp-ZH上达到39.1%。加入上下文管理后，性能分别提升至55.3%和52.9%。该结果显著优于同等4B规模的搜索智能体，并可匹配约30B规模模型的表现。

Conclusion: 论文表明，基于答案回溯的步骤级信用分配能够有效识别并强化长程搜索过程中的有用行为，同时抑制错误和冗余动作。该方法在有限训练数据和较小模型规模下仍获得显著性能提升，说明稠密、细粒度的过程监督是训练高效长程搜索智能体的重要方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon search agents must make multiple sequential actions (steps) to search, retrieve, verify, and integrate evidence to reach a final answer. However, existing methods for training these agents typically treat all steps within a trajectory uniformly during both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), failing to distinguish useful actions from erroneous or redundant ones. In this paper, we propose Answer-Backtracked Credit Assignment (ABC), a fine-grained credit assignment framework for training long-horizon search agents by converting sparse trajectory-level outcomes into dense step-level supervision that rewards useful actions (even in failed trajectories) while suppressing erroneous or redundant actions. Specifically, given a potentially obscure query and its corresponding ground-truth answer, ABC first performs Answer-Backtracked Clue Recovery, which traces back from the answer to recover intermediate clues required to solve the question. It then applies Clue-Anchored Step Scoring to evaluate each search step against these clues, converting sparse binary outcome supervision into dense step-level rewards. Based on these rewards, we develop ABC-SFT, which reweights the loss of each turn, and ABC-GRPO, which uses the step-level scores as rewards in GRPO. Building on this framework, we train ABSeeker based on Qwen3.5-4B with only 8.5k examples. ABSeeker achieves 37.3% on BrowseComp and 39.1% on BrowseComp-ZH. With context management, the scores further improve to 55.3% and 52.9%, respectively, significantly outperforming same-scale (4B) agents and even matching the performance of larger ones (approximately 30B). These results demonstrate the effectiveness of answer-backtracked step-level credit assignment for training long-horizon search agents.

</details>


### [70] [Argus: A General-Purpose Agentic Runtime for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05144)
*Boxiu Li,Zimo Wen,Yijia Fan,Junxiang Lei,Sufeng Guo,Jiaao Wu,Ruize Tang,Mukai Li,Yifei Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Wanbo Zhang,Runjing Gu,Yifei Gao,Yuheng Wu,Xuyao Huang,Zelong Zhao,Jiachen Zhang,Shibo Hu,Hangxi Guo,Yilin Chen,Yuzhe Zhang,Fan Yang,Chuan Wen,Xian Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Argus：一种固定模型权重、通过持久化运行时状态和控制策略实现“自我演化”的多智能体执行框架。其在多个基准上优于直接调用式基线，并显示出随经验积累而降低求解成本的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程推理任务要求系统既能在证据支持时持续推进，也能在发现失败、隐含约束或目标设定错误时及时转向。传统单次调用或短上下文代理缺乏跨任务持久记忆、可验证的经验沉淀以及明确的人工升级边界。

Method: Argus由Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四类角色组成，在持久化项目状态上执行有界任务。系统将稳定的用户意图与操作目标、约束和验证标准分离；记忆、技能、流程、验证器、路由决策及被拒绝路线，只有经角色负责审查且尽可能通过任务原生验证后才能写入。模型参数保持不变，系统通过运行时状态、验证门控的经验更新和控制策略调整实现自我演化，并在预设的人工接管节点之间自主运行。

Result: 在七个GPT-5.5评测场景中，Argus在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%，高于Direct Copilot的59%，但总令牌消耗为后者的1.41倍。经验证门控的自我演化后，成熟阶段相较启动阶段每任务求解输入令牌减少21%，活跃工作流时间减少15%；系统记录了34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环挽救。在AARRI-Bench上达到76.8%，数学数据合成任务领先28.0个百分点，并在GPU内核与语言模型训练任务中具竞争力。作者还报告了上游合并的RWKV6优化内核、多日数学任务中的已证伪路线保留，以及六条论文流水线完成254项任务和16次阶段回滚。

Conclusion: Argus表明，无需更新模型权重，也可借助持久状态、角色分工、验证门控和可追溯失败记录，构建能够持续修正、恢复并积累已验证策略的代理运行时。其核心贡献在于将自我演化限定为可审查、可验证的运行时学习过程；但性能提升伴随更高总体令牌开销，且结果主要依赖所报告基准与工程案例，仍需进一步验证泛化性、成本效益和评测可复现性。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires an agentic runtime that can persist when evidence supports its current approach and pivot when measurements reveal failure, hidden constraints, or a misspecified objective. We present Argus, a persistent, self-evolving runtime in which Manager, Planner, Engineer, and Reviewer execute bounded missions over durable project state. Argus separates stable user intent from operational objectives, constraints, and verification criteria, and admits memories, skills, procedures, verifiers, routing decisions, and rejected routes only after role-owned review and, when available, task-native verification. Model weights remain fixed; self-evolution occurs through persistent runtime state and control policy, with autonomous execution between operator-owned escalation points. Across seven GPT-5.5 benchmark arenas, Argus achieves about 78% on SWE-Bench Pro versus 59% for Direct Copilot while using 1.41 times the aggregate tokens. After verification-gated self-evolution, mature SWE-Bench waves use 21% fewer solve-input tokens and 15% less active workflow time per task than startup waves, while recording 34 verifier recoveries and 22 strict review-loop rescues. Argus also reaches 76.8% on AARRI-Bench and a 28.0-point gap on mathematical data synthesis, with competitive GPU-kernel and language-model-training results. Beyond benchmarks, an optimized RWKV6 kernel was merged upstream; a multi-day mathematics campaign retained falsified routes and proof-backed frontier updates; and six paper pipelines completed 254 missions with 16 stage rollbacks. These results show that a fixed-weight, self-evolving harness can revise, recover, and accumulate verified approaches while producing structured trajectories for future supervised and reinforcement learning.

</details>

### [71] [Multi-Mode Debugging for FRP-Based Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04264)
*Yugo Otani,Sosuke Moriguchi,Takuo Watanabe*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出面向 Emfrp 嵌入式应用的多模式调试框架，通过将 Emfrp 源级抽象映射到编译后的 C/C++ 程序，缩小函数式响应式编程逻辑与底层可执行代码之间的调试鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Emfrp 程序会被编译为 C，并与平台相关的 C/C++ 输入输出组件结合。开发者通常只能使用 GDB 等传统调试器调试混合代码，难以直接观察时间变化值等 Emfrp 核心抽象，导致源程序逻辑与实际调试对象之间存在明显的抽象层级不匹配。

Method: 作者设计多模式调试框架：一方面支持在 Emfrp 抽象层级检查和调试响应式程序逻辑，另一方面保留对平台相关 C/C++ I/O 实现的检查能力。核心技术是源代码映射，将 Emfrp 的语言构造与编译后程序中的相应代码位置建立对应关系，从而使常规调试机制能够关联回 Emfrp 源级语义。

Result: 在 ESP32 微控制器上的案例研究中，作者使用具有代表性的调试场景评估该框架。结果表明，该方法可提升 Emfrp 嵌入式应用的调试效率，并帮助开发者在高级响应式逻辑和底层平台代码之间切换定位问题。

Conclusion: 该工作通过源代码映射和多层级调试支持，为 Emfrp 提供了兼顾语言抽象与平台代码的调试方案。其主要价值在于降低 FRP 嵌入式应用调试的认知与定位成本；但摘要未提供量化指标、映射开销、调试功能覆盖范围及与其他调试方案的详细对比。

Abstract: Emfrp is a functional reactive programming (FRP) language designed for small-scale embedded systems. Time-varying values are the primary abstraction mechanism in FRP and enable concise descriptions of reactive behavior. In practice, however, Emfrp programs are compiled into C and combined with platform-dependent input/output components written in C or C++. Consequently, developers must debug the resulting mixed C/C++ program using conventional debuggers such as GDB, even though the application logic is written in Emfrp. This situation creates an abstraction gap between the source-level FRP program and the executable system. This paper presents a multi-mode debugging framework for Emfrp-based embedded applications. The framework supports debugging at the level of Emfrp abstractions while also allowing inspection of platform-specific C/C++ I/O code. Our approach uses a source code mapping technique that relates Emfrp constructs to corresponding locations in the compiled program. A case study on an ESP32 microcontroller using representative debugging scenarios demonstrates improved debugging efficiency.

</details>


### [72] [Accelerating C/C++ Pointer Analysis via Compiler-Based Offline Simplifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04466)
*Zinan Gu,Peisen Yao,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出在指针分析前直接对中间表示（IR）施加语义保持的编译器优化，以替代依赖特定算法的离线简化方法；实验表明该策略可获得最高3.14倍加速和1.94倍内存降低，且分析精度基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指针分析是优化、程序切片、缺陷检测与验证等静态分析任务的基础，但其成本通常较高。现有离线简化技术往往与特定指针分析算法紧密耦合，并受限于预定义的简化规则，因此缺少通用性、模块化能力和工具集成便利性。

Method: 作者将语义保持的编译器优化前置到指针分析流程，在执行指针分析之前优化程序IR。该方法不依赖具体指针分析算法，可与现有分析工具集成。论文在多样化程序集上，针对三种指针分析开展实证评估，并进一步考察优化开销与分析加速之间的权衡、IR结构变化、不同优化配置的特征及其效果。

Result: 实验结果显示，IR预优化能够显著改善指针分析效率：最高达到3.14倍运行时间加速，并将内存使用最高降低1.94倍。与此同时，指针分析精度整体上基本保持不变，说明优化并未明显削弱分析结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 在指针分析前应用通用编译器IR优化是一种有效、模块化且分析无关的性能提升路径。其价值在于复用成熟编译器优化基础设施，并避免为不同分析算法分别设计专用简化机制。后续研究可进一步探索优化配置选择、优化成本控制，以及更适合提升静态分析性能的IR变换策略。

Abstract: Pointer analysis is a cornerstone of numerous static analysis applications, including compiler optimizations, slicing, bug detection, and verification. While offline simplification is a common approach to boosting performance, existing methods are often tightly coupled to specific analysis algorithms and limited to a set of simplification rules. This paper explores a new perspective: applying semantic-preserving compiler optimizations directly to intermediate representation (IR) before pointer analysis. This strategy is modular, analysis-agnostic, and easily integrates with existing tools. We conduct an empirical study using diverse programs and three pointer analyses. The results show substantial performance gains---up to 3.14x speedup and 1.94x memory reduction---while precision remains largely unchanged. We also analyze the trade-offs between optimization overhead and analysis speedup, quantify changes in IR structure, assess the characteristics of optimization configurations, and identify promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Datalog on Quantum Annealers: Compiling Recursive Logic Programs with Bottom-up Semantics to 2-local Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04645)
*Bruno Rucy Carneiro Alves de Lima,Victor Henrique Cabral Pinheiro,Evgenii Dolzhkov,Joseph Haske*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出将递归Datalog程序编译为量子退火器可求解的2-局部Ising模型，并通过Lean 4形式化验证证明：若获得编译模型的基态，其在原子变量上的投影即为原程序的最小Herbrand模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归Datalog的最小模型语义是逻辑编程与演绎数据库的重要基础。作者旨在探索量子退火硬件是否能够作为该类逻辑推理任务的求解平台，同时弥合“编译正确性已证明”与“硬件实际能否找到目标基态”之间的差距。

Method: 编译流程包含四个阶段：二元化、实例化、归约为Min-Ones SAT公式，以及Ising编码。对每条规则，构造一个仅在违反该规则的赋值上产生能量惩罚的项；再对所有取真的原子施加统一的小代价，以在满足规则的模型中选择真原子数最少的最小模型。论文为各阶段给出正确性引理，并在Lean 4中验证整体对应定理。随后将得到的Ising模型映射到商用量子退火器的硬件拓扑，并使用经典退火和模拟量子退火考察目标基态的可达性。

Result: 理论结果表明，编译后Hamiltonian的基态投影与输入递归Datalog程序的最小Herbrand模型一致；该结论由逐阶段正确性证明和Lean 4形式化验证支撑。实验部分的目标是刻画不同模型与嵌入条件下，经经典退火及模拟量子退火是否、以及在何种条件下能够达到这一经认证的基态。摘要未提供具体实例规模、成功率、时间成本或与基线方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一条从递归Datalog最小模型语义到量子退火Ising优化的经形式验证编译链路。其核心价值在于保证“基态正确”而非仅凭经验构造编码；实际适用性则取决于硬件拓扑映射、嵌入开销及退火过程找到全局基态的能力，仍需结合完整实验数据评估其规模化优势。

Abstract: Quantum annealers solve problems by finding the lowest-energy (ground) state of a programmable physical system, a 2-local Ising model, whose energy function is the Hamiltonian. We compile recursive Datalog programs into such models so that the ground state projects onto the program's minimal Herbrand model. The compiler has four stages: binarization, grounding, reduction to a Min-Ones SAT formula, and Ising encoding. Each rule becomes an energy penalty on the one assignment that violates it, and a small uniform cost on every true atom selects the minimal model. We contribute both in theory and in practice with per-stage correctness lemmas and a correspondence theorem, verified in Lean 4, establishing that the ground state of the compiled model projects onto the program's minimal Herbrand model. We map the compiled models onto the topologies of commercial annealers and characterize, under classical and simulated-quantum annealing, whether and when that certified ground state is attained.

</details>

*Amit Singha,Ziqian Bi,Tao Li,Yimin Chen,Yanchao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Wireless Human Activity Recognition (HAR), leveraging their non-intrusive nature, has the potential to revolutionize various sectors, including healthcare, virtual reality, and surveillance. The advent of millimeter wave (mmWave) technology has significantly enhanced the capabilities of wireless HAR systems. This paper presents the first systematic study on the vulnerabilities of mmWave-based HAR to label flipping poisoning attacks in the context of supervised contrastive learning. We identify three label poisoning attacks on the contrastive mmWave-based HAR and propose corresponding countermeasures. The efficacy of the attacks and also our countermeasures are experimentally validated on a prototype system. The attacks and countermeasures can be easily extended to other wireless HAR systems, thereby promoting security considerations in system design and deployment.

</details>


### [75] [SoK: How Frontier AI Reshapes System-Level Security Risk Dynamics in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04033)
*Chandra Thapa,Mohan Baruwal Chhetri,Marthie Grobler,Shahroz Tariq,Tooba Aamir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向关键基础设施中前沿人工智能（FAI）部署的系统性风险框架，主张从单一模型鲁棒性转向覆盖全生命周期、跨系统依赖与运行治理的系统级安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全研究多按攻击类型、生命周期阶段或资产类别分类，难以解释FAI在关键基础设施中引发的风险如何出现、渗透、扩散并失控。FAI的大规模通用能力、供应链嵌入性和快速决策特征，也削弱了传统安全假设，如网络隔离、组件可解释性及人工可及时干预性。

Method: 论文采用系统化知识综述（SoK）方式，构建五维风险动态框架：能力涌现、渗透路径、跨系统传播、控制权弱化和响应能力承压。该框架以风险机制而非威胁清单为分析单位，覆盖FAI接入、运行和治理的完整生命周期，并据此比较学术研究重点与关键基础设施实际运营约束之间的差异。

Result: 研究指出，FAI风险并非由孤立漏洞决定，而是由新型攻防能力、数据与模型供应链入口、基础设施间依赖关系、技术与人工控制失效，以及机构在高压运行下的处置能力不足共同塑造。论文进一步发现，当前学术AI安全研究与关键基础设施部署需求存在结构性错配：前者偏重模型层面的稳健性，后者更需要可验证的系统级保障、运行控制和协同响应能力。

Conclusion: 关键基础设施中的FAI安全应采用生命周期结构化和系统级保障范式。未来研究应围绕部署导向的保证标准展开，包括供应链可追溯性、跨系统传播约束、可执行的人机控制机制、持续监测与事件响应韧性，从而弥合模型安全研究与真实运营环境之间的差距。

Abstract: Frontier artificial intelligence (FAI), encompassing large-scale, general-purpose AI systems, including large language models, multimodal foundation models, and agentic systems, is increasingly integrated into critical infrastructure (CI). This challenges long-standing security assumptions of bounded behavior, segmented networks, component transparency, and human-paced decision-making. Existing AI-security literature typically organizes risks by attack type, lifecycle stage, or asset class, but fails to capture the system-level dynamics, such as how risk emerges, spreads, and is controlled, through which FAI reshapes CI security outcomes. This Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) introduces a five-dimensional risk-dynamics framework that characterizes how FAI reconfigures CI security across the lifecycle: (i) Capability Emergence through new FAI-enabled attack and defense capabilities, (ii) Infiltration Pathways through data, models and AI supply chains, (iii) Cross-System Propagation across interconnected infrastructures and dependencies, (iv) degradation of effective technical and human Control Authority, and (v) strain on institutional Response Capacity under operational pressure. Rather than enumerating threats, the framework identifies recurring mechanisms that jointly determine system-level risk. We further identify a structural mismatch between academic AI-security research and CI operational constraints, and derive a deployment-oriented research agenda grounded in system-level assurance criteria. Collectively, this work shifts attention from model-centric robustness to lifecycle-structured, system-level assurance in interconnected CI environments.

</details>


### [76] [A Multimodal Automatic Redteaming Evaluation based on Atomic Jailbreak Strategy Decoupling and Combination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04034)
*Shiji Zhao,Yuxuan Zhou,Chen Xiong,Dongxian Wu,Yang Bai,Xun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出HACA，一种面向多模态大语言模型的自动化越狱红队测试方法，通过分层原子策略组合生成图文越狱指令，并在五个主流模型上取得平均95.48%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态越狱方法主要依赖人工经验，缺乏细粒度、可组合的图文联合策略空间，也缺少高效统一的自动化执行框架，因而难以系统覆盖不同类型的安全风险。

Method: 作者将图文越狱策略分解为结构、语义和句法三个层级，并覆盖文本与图像两种模态，形成六维策略空间。HACA通过跨模态联合规划器选择并组合原子策略，再由统一生成执行器依据所选策略直接生成越狱指令。

Result: 实验表明，HACA针对五个主流多模态大语言模型的平均攻击成功率达到95.48%，显示出较强的自动化攻击生成与跨模型迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该工作将多模态越狱从依赖人工构造的攻击方式转化为可分解、可组合和可自动执行的红队评测流程。其高成功率说明当前多模态模型仍存在显著安全脆弱性，也表明该框架可用于加强安全评估与防御研究。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved impressive progress in image-text comprehension and generation, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that can trigger harmful outputs and pose serious safety concerns. Existing multimodal jailbreak attacks have shown the feasibility of such attacks, but they still face two fundamental challenges: the lack of a atomic multi-modal strategy space, the absence of a concise and efficient executable framework beyond human-craft experience. To address these challenges, we first decompose the text-image jailbreak strategy space into three levels: structural, semantic, and syntactic, constructing a jailbreak strategy set encompassing both text and image modalities to systematically achieve combined coverage of different attack types. Then we propose a multimodal automated red team jailbreak method named Hierarchical Atomic Combination Attack (HACA). Specifically, based on a six-dimensional strategy space, a cross-modal joint planner is used to select and combine the different atomic jailbreak strategy for subsequent jailbreak command generation. Finally, at the implementation level, we explore to apply a unified generate executor to directly generate jailbreak instructions based on the selected multi-modal strategies. A series of experiments show that our automated red team method can achieve an attack success rate of average 95.48\% against five mainstream MLLMs.

</details>


### [77] [AMD SEV-SNP: A Confidential Computing Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04039)
*Amean Asad,Patrick McClurg,Patrick Woodhead*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统介绍 AMD SEV-SNP：一种面向虚拟机的硬件机密计算技术，旨在即使虚拟机监控器不可信的条件下，仍保护客户虚拟机的内存、寄存器状态与运行身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟化依赖虚拟机监控器保障客户虚拟机安全，但监控器具备高权限，一旦不可信或被攻陷，可能读取、修改或伪造客户虚拟机状态。SEV-SNP 的目标是在该高权限软件不受信任时，为虚拟机提供隔离、完整性保护及可验证的运行证明。

Method: 文章从硬件基础与威胁模型出发，说明 AMD 安全处理器及内存控制器加密引擎如何保护虚拟机状态；利用反向映射表（RMP）约束物理页面映射并防止页面表控制方篡改内存；通过虚拟机特权级、加密虚拟机保存区（VMSA）和 GHCB 通信协议，实现客户机与不可信监控器之间的受控协作；最后通过远程证明流程，将客户机初始状态度量值与 AMD 证书链绑定。

Result: SEV-SNP 能使监控器无法获得客户机内存和寄存器状态的加密密钥，并在客户机访问内存时检测篡改行为。RMP 提供针对恶意页表操控的内存完整性保障；特权与通信机制维持虚拟化运行所需的交互；证明机制使远程验证方能够独立确认客户机实际加载和运行的初始代码与状态。

Conclusion: 该文将 SEV-SNP 的安全保证分解为硬件加密、内存完整性、受控特权交互和远程证明四个层面，表明其可在不信任虚拟机监控器的场景下，为虚拟机提供可信执行环境能力。

Abstract: This paper is a technical primer on AMD Secure Encrypted Virtualization with Secure Nested Paging (SEV-SNP), a hardware confidential computing implementation that provides Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) for virtual machines. SEV-SNP treats the hypervisor as adversarial. It encrypts guest memory and register state with keys the hypervisor never possesses, detects any tampering with guest memory at the point of access, and lets a guest prove to a remote verifier exactly what code it is running. The paper constructs each of these guarantees from the hardware up. It opens with the threat model that drives the design and the hardware that enforces it, the AMD Secure Processor and the encryption engine in the memory controller. It then develops the mechanisms that make a confidential guest practical. The Reverse Map Table provides memory integrity against an adversary who controls the page tables. The privilege and communication machinery (VM Privilege Levels, the encrypted VM Save Area, and the GHCB protocol) lets the guest cooperate with a hypervisor it does not trust. The attestation pipeline binds a hardware-signed measurement of the guest's initial state to AMD's certificate chain, so a remote verifier can confirm independently what is running.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond the QBER Threshold: A Temporal QBER Based Machine Learning Framework for Multi Attack Detection in BB84 QKD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04047)
*Isha,Deepak Singh,Devesh Kumar,S. K Pal,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于时间序列QBER特征的机器学习框架，用于在BB84量子密钥分发系统中检测并分类隐蔽窃听攻击。相较固定11% QBER阈值，该方法显著降低漏检率，并兼具多类别识别能力与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BB84监测通常以会话平均QBER是否超过11%作为安全判据，但部分隐蔽攻击能够将误码率维持在阈值以下，仍可能破坏信道安全。因此，需要利用QBER随时间变化所反映的突发性、波动性及与损耗的耦合关系，提升对低可见性攻击的检测能力。

Method: 作者构建了物理信息驱动的时间QBER特征集，共提取63项特征，覆盖误码突发行为、时间稳定性、不同基之间的不对称性，以及QBER与信道损耗的交互关系。在含噪声和损耗条件下，对正常信道及7类攻击场景进行建模，比较随机森林、XGBoost和RBF核支持向量机的分类效果；同时将模型作为二元“攻击—正常”检测器，与固定11%阈值方法比较。最后采用SHAP分析关键特征的贡献。

Result: 在10次独立实验的平均结果中，XGBoost表现最佳，准确率为88.01%（标准差0.47%），宏平均F1为0.8803；SVM-RBF结果相近，说明特征具有较好的稳健性。二元检测中，固定11%阈值的准确率仅为25.82%，漏检率为0.8477；所提框架将漏检率降至0.0198，表明其对可规避阈值检测的隐蔽攻击更敏感。SHAP结果进一步显示，时间动态特征和信道派生特征对攻击策略区分具有较高判别力。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，基于时间QBER而非会话平均QBER的机器学习监测方式，能够有效识别多类隐蔽攻击，并显著优于固定阈值监测。其主要贡献在于将物理可解释的时序特征、监督分类器和SHAP解释结合，为BB84系统提供更准确、可解释且具备实践潜力的安全监控方案。仍需在更贴近真实部署的条件下进一步验证，例如不同设备漂移、有限样本统计效应、攻击分布变化及实时计算开销。

Abstract: Conventional BB84 Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) systems rely on a fixed 11% Quantum Bit Error Rate (QBER) threshold to detect eavesdropping. However, stealthy attacks can remain below this threshold while still compromising channel security. This paper proposes a temporal QBER based machine learning framework for detecting and classifying eavesdropping attacks in BB84 QKD systems. Rather than relying on average session level QBER, the framework extracts 63 physics-informed temporal features capturing burst behavior, temporal instability, basis dependent asymmetry, and QBER loss interactions. Random Forest, XGBoost, and Support Vector Machine with a Radial Basis Function kernel (SVM-RBF) classifiers are evaluated on seven eavesdropping attacks and a normal channel scenario under noisy and lossy conditions. Averaged over ten independent runs, XGBoost achieves the best performance with 88.01% (0.47%) accuracy and a macro F1 score of 0.8803, while SVM-RBF performs comparably, confirming the robustness of the proposed features. Evaluated as a binary attack-versus-normal detector for comparison with conventional monitoring, a fixed 11% QBER threshold achieves only 25.82% accuracy with a False Negative Rate (FNR) of 0.8477, whereas the proposed framework reduces the FNR to 0.0198, substantially improving detection of stealthy attacks that evade threshold-based monitoring. SHapley Additive exPlanations based (SHAP) explainability shows that physics-informed temporal and channel derived features are highly discriminative for identifying eavesdropping strategies. These results demonstrate that temporal QBER driven machine learning provides an accurate, explainable, and practical framework for multi attack security monitoring in BB84 QKD systems.

</details>


### [79] [When Modalities Fail to Tango: Conformal Backdoor Detection in Multimodal Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04052)
*Yiming Chen,Kemou Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出CASCADE，一种面向多模态对比学习投毒后门样本检测的两阶段保序预测框架。该方法通过粗粒度跨模态一致性筛选与细粒度文本空间相似性判别，在CC3M数据集上取得较低误报率和较高检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测方法主要以CLIPScore作为图文语义一致性指标，并采用固定阈值区分正常与投毒样本。但正常样本和投毒样本的CLIPScore分布存在显著重叠，导致单一指标的区分能力有限；同时，固定阈值无法为重叠区域中的模糊样本提供统计置信保证。因此，需要一种能够显式量化检测不确定性、并对难判样本进行可靠识别的方法。

Method: CASCADE基于保序预测，将样本检测划分为粗到细两个阶段。粗粒度阶段利用图像与文本之间的跨模态一致性，识别高置信度的正常样本和投毒样本。细粒度阶段以高置信度投毒样本构建参考集，对未确定样本计算基于文本嵌入空间相似性的实例级非一致性分数。该分数衡量待测样本与投毒分布之间的符合程度，并借助保序预测提供的统计置信界，识别隐藏在不确定集合中的投毒图文对。

Result: 在大规模CC3M数据集及多种攻击设定下，CASCADE平均实现100%检测率时5.79%的误报率，并获得0.9867的平均AUROC。实验还表明，该方法对自适应攻击仍保持有效性，说明其并非仅依赖静态攻击模式。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将保序预测引入多模态对比学习的投毒样本检测，以统计化不确定性量化替代单纯固定阈值决策。CASCADE通过高置信样本锚定投毒参考分布，再对模糊样本进行细粒度判别，缓解了CLIPScore分布重叠造成的检测困难，并展现出较强的准确性、低误报特征与适应性。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks in multimodal contrastive learning (MCL) have garnered growing attention in recent years, as many downstream tasks critically depend on pre-trained MCL models. Existing detection-based defenses predominantly rely on the CLIPScore metric, under the assumption that poisoned pairs exhibit lower semantic similarity between the image and the caption. However, we identify two critical flaws remaining in existing methods: (1) the substantial overlap between CLIPScore distributions of benign and poisoned pairs undermines the reliability of this metric, and (2) fixed-threshold detection cannot provide statistical guarantees for ambiguous samples within overlapping regions. To overcome these limitations, we propose integrating conformal prediction (CP), a statistical framework that quantifies uncertainty through nonconformity scores (NCSs), to establish provable confidence bounds for detecting poisoned image-caption pairs. Building on CP, we introduce CASCADE, a novel two-stage Coarse-to-Fine Conformal Backdoor Detection framework. The coarse-grained stage uses cross-modality consistency to identify high-confidence benign and poisoned pairs. In the fine-grained stage, a reference set is constructed from high-confidence poisoned pairs, and instance-level NCSs based on text-space similarity are computed for each sample in the unidentified subset. These NCSs measure conformity to the poisoning distribution and enable precise identification of latent poisoned pairs within the unidentified subset. Extensive experiments on the large-scale CC3M dataset demonstrate that CASCADE achieves an average FPR of 5.79% at 100% TPR and an average AUROC of 0.9867 across diverse attacks, while remaining effective against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [80] [AgentAntibody: An Adaptive Immune System for Defending LLM Agents against Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04053)
*Shihao Weng,Yang Feng,Xiaofei Xie,Jiongchi Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentAntibody：一种面向LLM智能体提示注入防御的持续学习框架。该方法通过跨任务积累用户可接受行为边界，将历史经验沉淀为持久化“抗体库”，以识别并应对后续任务中的相似攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入防御通常将每个任务视为彼此独立的问题，无法利用历史交互中逐渐明确的用户偏好与安全边界。由于用户请求常存在行为约束上的欠定义，攻击者可利用这种歧义诱导智能体执行虽然符合字面任务、但违背用户真实意图的操作。因此，需要一种能够从具体案例中持续学习并迁移安全经验的防御机制。

Method: AgentAntibody借鉴自适应免疫系统，将对用户安全边界的理解表示为可持续更新的抗体库。运行时，系统利用抗体库识别当前请求或上下文中可能突破该边界的威胁，并触发相应的防御响应；在完成新的交互后，系统根据案例进一步更新抗体库，从而增强未来面对类似攻击时的防御能力。其核心特点是将防御知识跨任务持久化，而非仅依赖单次推理或固定规则。

Result: 论文在三个基准测试和四种骨干大语言模型上进行了实验。结果表明，AgentAntibody能够通过历史经验学习用户安全边界，在阻止不符合用户预期的操作方面优于已有防御方法，同时较好保留合法任务的完成能力。该优势尤其体现在有害与合法操作均可能与任务字面描述相容的模糊场景。

Conclusion: AgentAntibody将提示注入防御从静态、单任务的检测问题扩展为面向用户安全边界的持续学习问题。通过持久化抗体库和跨交互更新机制，该方法有望提升LLM智能体在真实、欠定义任务环境中的长期安全性与任务可用性。

Abstract: Prompt injection remains a critical threat to LLM agents, yet existing defenses treat each task as a self-contained problem, independent of previous encounters. In practice, user requests are often underspecified: they describe the desired outcome without fully specifying acceptable behavior. An injection can exploit this ambiguity, causing the agent to complete the task in a way the user would reject. As the user's expectations become clearer through concrete cases, a defense should learn from each encounter and apply what it learns to the next. Inspired by adaptive immunity, we propose AgentAntibody, which equips LLM agents with a self-evolving immune system against prompt injection. AgentAntibody represents its evolving understanding of the user's security boundary as a persistent library of antibodies. At runtime, the library recognizes threats to this boundary and mounts corresponding immune responses. Across encounters, it evolves to strengthen the agent's immunity to future attacks. Extensive experiments across three benchmarks and four backbone LLMs show that, by learning the user's boundary through experience, AgentAntibody outperforms existing defenses in preventing harmful actions while preserving legitimate task completion, even when the harmful and legitimate actions are both compatible with the stated task.

</details>


### [81] [An Inline Control Architecture for Language Models in Intelligent Transportation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04065)
*Narendra Kumar Dewangan,Mounira Msahli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Guarded-V2X，一种面向LLM增强型车联网语义服务的在线安全护栏架构。该方法通过多层输入过滤、分类、受约束生成、可信检索与结果裁决，降低提示注入等攻击带来的风险，并满足实时语义辅助场景的时延要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X安全机制主要保护身份认证、消息完整性和通信链路，但难以防范LLM组件面临的提示级攻击。尽管LLM不直接参与安全关键控制，其在路侧单元和边缘节点中承担摘要、辅助决策和运营支持等任务时，仍可能输出不安全或被操纵的语义结果，因此需要适配实时约束的语义安全防护机制。

Method: Guarded-V2X采用内联式多层护栏：首先以规则引擎进行入口过滤；随后使用轻量级安全分类器识别高风险请求；在生成阶段施加策略约束并输出结构化结果；检索模块仅使用可信数据源；最终通过生成后裁决机制验证结果是否满足可机器检查的安全边界。实验流程包括漏洞分析、校准与时延测试、护栏有效性验证，以及多轮对抗压力下的鲁棒性评估；数据集由路侧单元通告、运营人员消息和标注后的V2X消息摘要构成。

Result: 实验表明，未部署护栏的系统和仅依赖提示词的基线方法，在多轮对抗测试中仍存在残余攻击成功率。Guarded-V2X能够稳定降低攻击输入被接受的比例，并在双轮交互测试中未观察到不安全输出。同时，该架构的推理与防护开销未超过V2X语义建议链路的时延预算。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，传统通信安全机制之外，还需要针对LLM语义层引入专门防护。Guarded-V2X通过分层、可验证且面向实时性的安全设计，为车联网中LLM辅助服务提供了有效的提示攻击缓解方案。摘要的主要局限是未给出具体数据规模、攻击成功率下降幅度、时延数值及与更多先进防护方法的定量对比，因此其泛化性和实际部署成本仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-everything (V2X) systems increasingly incorporate large language models (LLMs) for semantic tasks such as message summarization, operator assistance, and decision support at roadside units and edge nodes. Although these components are not part of safety-critical control loops, they introduce prompt-level attack surfaces that are not addressed by traditional V2X security mechanisms focused on authentication and message integrity. This paper presents Guarded-V2X, an inline semantic guardrail architecture for securing LLM-enabled V2X services under real-time constraints. The proposed system integrates rule-based ingress filtering, a lightweight safety classifier, policy-constrained structured generation, trusted-only retrieval, and post-decision adjudication to enforce machine-checkable safety boundaries prior to downstream execution. Guarded-V2X is evaluated using a four-stage experimental pipeline encompassing intrusion vulnerability analysis, calibration and latency benchmarking, guardrail validation, and robustness under adversarial stress. Experiments are conducted on a V2X-aligned simulated dataset derived from RSU advisories, operator messages, and annotated V2X message summaries. Results show that unguarded and prompt-only baselines retain residual vulnerability under multi-turn adversarial trials, while Guarded-V2X consistently reduces intrusion acceptance success rates and eliminates observed unsafe completions in two-turn settings, without exceeding latency budgets for V2X semantic advisory paths.

</details>


### [82] [FBID: Adaptive Personalized Federated Learning for Robust Out-of-Distribution Attack Detection in IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04073)
*An Khanh Bui,Cong Thanh Nguyen,Hoang-Anh Pham,Hoang Thai Dinh,Diep N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出FBID，一种面向异构物联网入侵检测的个性化联邦学习框架，通过服务器端上下文多臂老虎机动态调节客户端本地训练强度，并结合基于信任的全局—本地模型融合，以缓解过度个性化导致的分布外检测性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化联邦学习通常依赖客户端自行调整本地训练，虽然能够适应非独立同分布数据，但可能使模型过度贴合本地数据，从而遗忘全局检测知识，并显著降低对分布外或未见类别的检测能力。论文旨在在本地适应性与全局泛化能力之间实现更稳健的平衡。

Method: FBID在服务器端使用上下文多臂老虎机，根据客户端历史行为和模型更新质量，自适应决定其本地训练强度。随后，框架通过信任驱动的融合机制，为各客户端生成不同的全局模型与本地模型插值系数：高可信的本地更新可获得更多个性化权重，而需要保持泛化能力的客户端则保留更多全局知识。

Result: 在CICIoT2023数据集、异构客户端分布及分布外压力测试条件下，FBID相对于最强的稳定基线方法，单客户端分布外检测率最高提升7.66%，F1分数最高相对提升5.08%；同时，对此前未见类别的检测鲁棒性也有所增强。

Conclusion: FBID表明，个性化联邦学习不应仅由客户端独立控制。通过服务器端自适应训练调度和信任式模型融合，可以在维持本地专属适应能力的同时减少过度个性化，提高异构物联网场景下的分布外泛化与未知类别检测性能。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) has emerged as a promising solution for intrusion detection in heterogeneous IoT environments, as it can improve local adaptation under highly Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (non-IID) data distributions. However, existing PFL methods often rely on client-side self-adjustment, which may lead to over-personalization and substantial degradation in out-of-distribution (OOD) attack detection. In this paper, we propose Federated Bandit Intrusion Detection (FBID), a novel adaptive PFL framework to address this limitation through server-side personalization control. In particular, FBID employs a contextual multi-armed bandit at the server to dynamically regulate each client's local training intensity according to its observed behavior and update quality. Moreover, FBID introduces a trust-based blending mechanism to derive client-specific interpolation coefficients between the global and local models, thereby preserving global attack-detection knowledge while still allowing beneficial local specialization. Through extensive experiments on the CICIoT2023 dataset under heterogeneous client distributions and OOD stress-test settings, we show that FBID improves individual client OOD Detection Rate (DR) by up to 7.66% and F1-Score (F1) by up to 5.08% (relative) over the strongest stable baseline, while also improving robustness to previously unseen attack classes.

</details>


### [83] [NEBULA: A Language - Independent Specification for Opaque Rotating Refresh Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04115)
*Matteo Teodori*

Main category: cs.CR
