TL;DR: 论文提出NEBULA：针对RFC 9700中公开客户端刷新令牌轮换与重放检测要求的、与编程语言无关的精确定义，并提供十种语言的参考实现及机器可读一致性测试套件，以减少并发、重试和密钥轮换等边界情形中的实现分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RFC 9700规定公开客户端的刷新令牌必须在每次使用时轮换并进行重放检测，或采用发送者约束；但其未规定令牌格式、服务端存储结构、验证步骤顺序、并发语义及丢失响应后的重试处理。由此，不同实现可能在决定实际安全性的边界条件上产生差异，尤其可能出现并发刷新时绕过重用检测的问题。

Method: NEBULA采用不透明令牌设计：令牌由128位公开选择器和256位秘密校验值组成，均由密码学安全随机数生成器产生，不携带声明或签名；令牌有效性完全由服务端状态决定。规范定义了刷新、轮换、重用检测、重试及相关状态转换语义，并使用比较并交换（CAS）定义原子轮换契约。作者将38个行为场景编码为单一机器可读文件，各语言实现通过轻量运行器执行同一测试数据；同时提供TypeScript、Python、Go、Rust、Java、PHP、C#、Ruby、Elixir和Dart共十种参考实现。

Result: 论文声称CAS轮换契约能够封闭一个可复现的并发刷新漏洞：若缺少原子状态更新，多个并发请求可能同时接受同一旧刷新令牌，从而削弱或绕过重用检测。机器可读测试套件使跨语言实现共享相同的行为定义，降低自然语言规范转录导致的语义漂移。令牌的256位秘密校验值也为抗暴力猜测提供较高安全余量，并被作者用于讨论后量子安全态势。

Conclusion: NEBULA的主要贡献不是提出新的OAuth策略，而是将RFC 9700的原则性要求补全为可实现、可测试且可跨语言验证的操作规范。其核心价值在于明确服务端状态语义和并发原子性，并通过共享测试工件提升多实现生态中的一致性与安全性。摘要中的效果主要为规范设计与一致性方法的论证；对真实部署性能、存储成本、互操作性及复杂故障恢复场景的实证评估仍需结合全文确认。

Abstract: Refresh tokens are among the most sensitive credentials in modern authentication systems: long-lived, bearer-style, and sufficient to mint access tokens for days or weeks. RFC 9700, the current Best Current Practice for OAuth 2.0 security, mandates that refresh tokens issued to public clients be rotated on every use with replay (reuse) detection, or be sender-constrained. But the BCP specifies policy, not mechanism: it prescribes no wire format, no storage schema, no ordering of verification steps, no concurrency contract, and no semantics for edge cases such as lost-response retries or key rotation. Implementations may therefore diverge in precisely the corner cases that determine security outcomes.
  We present NEBULA, a precise, language-independent specification of the RFC 9700 refresh-token model, together with ten conformant reference implementations (TypeScript, Python, Go, Rust, Java, PHP, C#, Ruby, Elixir, Dart). NEBULA tokens are opaque -- a 128-bit public selector and a 256-bit secret verifier, both CSPRNG output, carrying no claims and no signature -- so token validity is a property of server-side state rather than of cryptographic verification. Its conformance methodology publishes the behavioural suite as data rather than as prose: 38 scenarios in one machine-readable file that every implementation executes through a thin per-language runner, so that drift by transcription is structurally excluded. We describe the specification -- including a compare-and-set rotation contract that closes a reproducible bypass of reuse detection under concurrent refresh -- analyse its security properties including its post-quantum posture, and report on cross-language conformance as a method for multi-implementation security specifications. The specification, implementations, and conformance artefacts are open source under the Apache License 2.0.

</details>


### [84] [Behavioral Information Leakage in Darknet Traffic: A Multi-Channel Analysis Across Anonymity Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04143)
*Javeriah Saleem,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出行为信息泄漏框架，用于解释加密匿名网络流量中不同服务为何仍可被区分，而非仅追求分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加密流量分类研究主要报告预测性能，缺少对可区分性的行为机制解释。作者旨在量化控制、结构和节律特征造成的信息泄漏，并比较不同匿名网络与服务类型的可分性及跨网络泛化能力。

Method: 将流级流量描述符划分为控制、结构和节律三类；使用归一化互信息衡量各特征组的信息泄漏，并以随机森林进行预测验证；进一步分析结构—节律交互作用、跨网络服务差异，并采用防泄漏的重复分层交叉验证。另设计服务变异指数和泄漏变异指数，用于比较不同服务在网络间的可分性变化。

Result: 不同匿名网络的行为泄漏差异显著。Tor的服务可分性最高，Macro-F1为0.7165，累计归一化泄漏为3.9461；FreeNet的综合泄漏最低，为0.8744。主要泄漏来源包括报文长度组织、双向通信不平衡、报文时间节奏及静默—突发行为。结构与节律特征联合表示在网络内分类中表现最佳，但留一网络外测试显示其跨匿名网络架构的迁移能力有限。视频服务呈现较稳定的网络特异性可分性，而聊天和邮件服务在不同网络对之间波动更大。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，加密流量的服务可识别性主要来自可观察的通信行为模式，而非内容本身。结构和节律特征的组合是最有效的网络内识别表示，但模型难以直接跨网络迁移。框架为解释加密流量分类结果、识别隐私泄漏来源以及评估不同网络架构的可区分性提供了量化工具。

Abstract: Existing darknet traffic classification studies largely emphasize predictive accuracy while offering limited insight into the behavioral mechanisms that make encrypted services distinguishable. This paper proposes a behavioral information leakage framework that decomposes flow-level traffic into control, structural, and rhythmic descriptor groups across Tor, I2P, FreeNet, and ZeroNet. The framework combines normalized mutual information analysis with Random Forest-based predictive validation, structural-rhythmic interaction analysis, and cross-network service-variability evaluation under leakage-safe repeated stratified cross-validation. Results show that behavioral leakage varies considerably across anonymity networks. Tor achieves the highest service separability, with a Macro-F1 of 0.7165 and cumulative normalized leakage of 3.9461, whereas FreeNet exhibits the lowest combined leakage of 0.8744. Packet-size organization, directional exchange imbalance, packet tempo, and silence-burst behavior emerge as the main leakage mechanisms. The combined structural-rhythmic representation consistently provides the strongest within-network performance, while leave-one-network-out evaluation reveals limited transferability across anonymity architectures. The proposed Service Variability Index and Leakage Variability Index further show that video exhibits consistent network-specific separability, whereas chat and email demonstrate greater variability across anonymity-network pairs.

</details>


### [85] [Open-World Darknet Traffic Recognition Under Leave-One-Service-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04167)
*Javeriah Saleem,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zahidul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，暗网流量分类在开放世界场景下性能显著下降，传统封闭世界评估高估了模型实际部署时的鲁棒性；未知服务与已知服务之间的行为相似性是核心难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有暗网流量识别研究通常假设训练和测试阶段的服务类别完全已知，但真实网络中会持续出现未见服务。因此，论文旨在构建更贴近实际的开放世界评估框架，并量化未知类别对分类模型可靠性的影响。

Method: 研究采用留一服务外（leave-one-service-out）协议：每次将一种服务完全排除于训练集，并在测试时作为未知类别出现。使用随机森林和XGBoost进行分类，并结合基于不确定性的拒识机制，以比较封闭世界与开放世界设定下的性能差异。另通过语义吸收分析，统计未知服务被错误归入哪些已知行为类别。

Result: 从封闭世界迁移至开放世界后，模型性能大幅降低。在I2P环境中，XGBoost的Macro-F1从88.8%降至46.1%，随机森林从87.4%降至45.7%。不确定性拒识仅带来有限改进，难以彻底解决未知流量被错误分类的问题。语义吸收结果显示，FreeNet视频流量有88.1%被归类为浏览流量，I2P点对点流量有83.4%被归入FTP相关行为，表明未知服务常因行为特征重叠而被系统性吸收至相似类别。

Conclusion: 论文证明封闭世界基准不能充分反映暗网流量分类器的真实部署能力。开放世界条件下，已知与未知服务的行为重叠会引发严重误判，而简单的不确定性拒识不足以保证可靠识别。后续研究应重点发展开放集识别、未知类检测、类别边界建模及面向行为相似性的鲁棒表征方法。

Abstract: Darknet traffic recognition is critical for cyber threat intelligence, as anonymity networks are often used to conceal malicious activity. However, most existing studies rely on closed-world evaluation, assuming all service categories are known during training and testing, which is unrealistic in real-world environments. This paper presents an open-world darknet traffic classification framework using leave-one-service-out evaluation and uncertainty-aware classification with Random Forest and XGBoost models. Experimental results demonstrate significant performance degradation when transitioning from closed-world to open-world settings, demonstrating that closed-world evaluation substantially overestimates deployment robustness. For example, XGBoost Macro-F1 decreases from 88.8% to 46.1% in the I2P environment, while Random Forest performance drops from 87.4% to 45.7%. Although uncertainty-based rejection slightly improves robustness, strong behavioral similarity between known and unknown services leads to frequent misclassification. Semantic absorption analysis further shows that FreeNet video traffic is classified as browsing traffic with an 88.1% assignment rate, while I2P peer-to-peer traffic is absorbed into FTP-related behavior with an 83.4% assignment rate. The findings demonstrate that behavioral overlap remains a major challenge for reliable open-world darknet traffic classification.

</details>


### [86] [Behavioral Skill Reconstruction: Reconstructing Hidden Functionality from LLM Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04192)
*Peichun Hua,Haoxuan Xu,Mengyuan Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究“行为式技能重构”（BSR）：即使闭源智能体技能的文件、提示词、脚本和数据不被直接泄露，用户仍可通过正常调用服务、观察输出并迭代测试，重建其功能性副本。作者提出黑盒攻击框架SkillClone，并表明仅保护文件秘密不足以保护功能秘密。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究与防御主要针对提示注入导致的文件、指令或内部工件泄露。但服务提供方即便成功隐藏这些文件，仍可能在持续响应合法请求的过程中暴露足够的行为信息，使用户复刻其实现的功能。因此，论文关注闭源技能能否在不发生文件披露的条件下被功能性重建。

Method: SkillClone首先根据目标技能的公开描述构建接口假设；随后发送结构化、良性的探测请求并收集响应；基于观察结果合成可执行副本；最后通过与目标技能进行差分验证，定位输出差异并迭代修复克隆实现。评估覆盖30个技能，包含规则、表格、流程和算法等类型，并使用留出输入测试重建副本的表现。

Result: 在多个目标上，SkillClone能够在留出测试输入上实现完全或部分功能恢复。相比单轮重建，迭代式重新查询和修复能够弥补初始推断遗漏的行为细节。由于攻击仅利用合法交互，针对文件泄露的传统防御覆盖有限；仅降低公开技能描述的详细程度也只能提供有限保护。

Conclusion: 闭源技能的文件保密不等同于功能保密。若服务持续对外提供可观察的输入—输出行为，攻击者可累积信息并重建其关键功能。防御策略除防止直接披露外，还需控制普通使用过程中的累计信息泄漏，例如限制查询、监测探测模式、降低可推断性，并评估接口行为暴露的风险。

Abstract: Closed source agent skills may encode proprietary instructions, scripts, constants, and data. Providers may offer their capabilities as services while keeping the underlying packages hidden. Prior work focuses on prompt injection attacks that directly disclose these artifacts, and existing defenses accordingly aim to prevent such leakage. However, preventing file disclosure does not prevent users from recovering the functionality those files implement. This raises a fundamental question: can a user reconstruct a skill's functionality through ordinary use while its files remain hidden?
  We study behavioral skill reconstruction (BSR), in which an attacker uses valid task requests and observed responses to build a functional clone of a hidden skill. We introduce SkillClone, a black-box attack that clones a target skill by forming an interface hypothesis from its public advertisement, issuing structured benign probes, synthesizing an executable replica, and iteratively repairing it through differential validation against the victim skill. Across 30 skills spanning rules, tables, procedures, and algorithms, SkillClone achieves exact or partial recovery on held-out inputs for several targets. Iterative requerying closes gaps missed by single-round reconstruction. Because SkillClone uses only legitimate interactions, disclosure-focused defenses provide limited coverage, and less detailed skill descriptions offer limited protection. These results show that file secrecy alone does not ensure functional secrecy. Defenses must also limit cumulative information leakage from ordinary use.

</details>


### [87] [PriDyG: Privacy-preserving Dynamic Graph Inference with LLM-GNN Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04255)
*Yuyang Xia,Ruixuan Liu,Li Xiong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出PriDyG，用于动态关系数据上的边级差分隐私推理。其核心是增量式私有多跳聚合：每条新增边仅处理一次，使总隐私开销不随模型更新次数或更新时机累积，并结合仅依赖节点文本的LLM语义预测提升效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图中的重复推理和模型更新会反复暴露边结构，导致隐私预算持续累积。现有按时间递减分配隐私预算的方法会在后续更新中引入越来越大的噪声，从而显著损害图神经网络的结构学习与预测性能。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为边级差分隐私动态图推理，并设计PriDyG框架。首先，GNN部分采用增量私有多跳聚合，对新到达边进行缓冲并保证每条边仅参与一次处理；借助差分隐私的并行组合性质，总隐私成本等同于一次静态发布。该机制保留精确的一跳信号，并至少保留二跳信息传递的一半。其次，框架引入基于节点文本的LLM预测作为语义补充；由于该模块不使用边信息，因此不会增加边级隐私成本。

Result: 在四个节点分类和链路预测基准上，PriDyG在相同隐私预算下稳定优于几何递减预算分配基线。其效用可达到朴素逐次更新重训练方法的水平，同时将累计隐私开销最多降低三个数量级。

Conclusion: PriDyG通过一次性处理新增边和并行组合机制，解决了动态图推理中隐私损失随更新累积的问题；结合不访问边结构的文本语义推理后，框架能在严格边级隐私约束下维持较高预测性能。该工作表明，面向动态图的隐私保护应优先设计可避免重复访问历史边的增量计算机制。

Abstract: Graph inference over relational data can expose sensitive edge information, and this risk becomes more severe in dynamic graphs, where repeated model updates cause privacy loss to accumulate. We formulate Edge-level Differentially Private Dynamic Graph Inference (EDG) and propose PriDyG, a private inference framework that combines GNN-based structural learning with LLM-based semantic reasoning. PriDyG introduces incremental private multi-hop aggregation, which buffers newly arrived edges and processes each edge exactly once. By parallel composition, the total privacy cost equals that of a single static release, independent of the number or schedule of model updates. Compared with geometrically decaying budget allocation, incremental aggregation avoids exponentially increasing noise while preserving exact one-hop signals and at least half of two-hop information transfers. PriDyG further complements privatized GNN outputs with LLM predictions derived solely from node text, incurring no additional edge-level privacy cost. Experiments on four benchmarks for node classification and link prediction show that PriDyG consistently outperforms geometrically decaying baselines under the same privacy budget and matches the utility of naive per-update retraining while reducing cumulative privacy cost by up to three orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [88] [Adversarial Attacks for Good: A Survey of Proactive Protection across the Visual Content Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04314)
*Jiaming Zhang,Boyang Chen,Zherui Li,Fuyao Zhang,Xinyu Yan,Hong Xi Tae,Wenwen He,Xuan Wang,Siqi Guo,Junhao Dong,Kun Wang,Hanxun Huang,Yige Li,Xingjun Ma,Yang Cao,Lingjuan Lyu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该综述提出“向善对抗攻击”范式：由视觉内容所有者、创作者、平台或审计者主动部署对抗扰动与结构化信号，以阻断未经授权的自动化处理或支持事后追责。论文统一梳理视觉资产生命周期中五类技术，并指出其真实部署证据和对强自适应对手的鲁棒性仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉内容一旦进入AI处理流程，所有者通常难以继续控制其被识别、训练、编辑、仿冒或传播后的使用方式。单靠法律与监管往往无法在技术层面及时阻止滥用，因此需要在内容发布、访问或流通的早期节点部署保护机制。现有相关研究分散于多个社区，目标、评估标准和术语不统一，缺乏跨阶段的系统比较。

Method: 论文将原本用于攻击学习模型的扰动和信号重新界定为保护工具，并按视觉资产生命周期归纳五类研究：分享时抵御非授权识别的隐私滤波器；阻止数据被用于未授权训练的不可学习样本；防范恶意编辑、生成或身份仿冒的生成式保护；阻止自动化代理访问的对抗性CAPTCHA；以及支持内容传播后归属与追责的溯源机制。作者进一步以迁移性、适应性和部署成熟度作为统一评价轴，对五类方法进行横向分析，并汇总跨阶段的防御策略与开放问题。

Result: 综述发现，五类技术虽然源于不同研究传统，但共同利用了人类感知、语义理解与机器推断之间持续存在的差距。多数方法在静态攻击者或弱适应性攻击者条件下效果较好，但面对会进行预处理、模型替换、再训练、查询优化或多模态协同的强自适应对手时，证据有限。许多结果仍局限于受控基准测试，跨模型迁移、长期稳定性、实际平台兼容性、用户体验成本及大规模部署可行性尚未充分验证。

Conclusion: “向善对抗攻击”可作为统一框架，连接视觉内容从发布、访问、训练、生成到传播追责的所有者侧保护技术。其核心价值在于将对抗性机制从破坏模型转向维护内容控制权；但要实现可靠应用，未来研究需强化针对自适应对手的评估，提升跨模型与跨模态迁移能力，并构建可组合、可审计、低负担且适合真实平台部署的端到端保护方案。

Abstract: Once visual content enters an AI pipeline, its owner often retains little technical control over how it is used. Legal and regulatory remedies can address misuse, but many technical interventions must be applied earlier, when content is released or accessed. This survey examines the protective paradigm that has grown around this intervention point, which we call \emph{adversarial attacks for good}. Perturbations and structured signals long studied as attacks on learned models are instead applied by data owners, creators, platforms, or auditors to disrupt unauthorized automation or support later accountability. Five research communities have arrived at this inversion largely independently, each addressing a different stage of a visual asset's lifecycle: privacy filters against unwanted recognition at sharing time, unlearnable examples against unauthorized training, generative safeguards against malicious editing or imitation, adversarial CAPTCHAs for access control against automated agents, and provenance mechanisms for post-circulation attribution. Although developed in separate venues with incompatible success criteria, many of these methods exploit persistent gaps between human perception, semantic interpretation, and machine inference, suggesting that the paradigm remains relevant as visual pipelines evolve toward multimodal models and autonomous agents. To make their claims comparable, we evaluate all five families along shared axes of transferability, adaptability, and deployment readiness. Across the lifecycle, we find that most protections are still validated mainly against static or weakly adaptive adversaries, while evidence beyond controlled benchmarks remains scarce. We close by consolidating cross-stage countermeasures and open problems for robust, composable, and deployable owner-side protection.

</details>


### [89] [Trident : How to Break Deep Reinforcement Learning Cyber Defenses (Agentic)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04317)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Ian Bryant,Armita Kazeminajafabadi,Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,SungHeon Jeong,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Trident，一个面向自适应网络威胁评估的 LLM 红队框架，结合动态基准环境、红蓝交互数据集与基于 RLVR 的 Code-as-Policy 智能体，用于检验 DRL 防御系统对自适应攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主网络防御研究主要使用静态、启发式红队代理，难以反映真实的自适应威胁；同时，RLVR 在网络安全领域缺乏合适的交互环境和训练数据，限制了其应用。

Method: Trident 包含三个部分：覆盖 CybORG CAGE 4 和 CyberWheel 的隔离沙箱动态基准、超过 13,000 条高保真红蓝交互轨迹的数据集，以及 Trident Agentic 架构。该架构将红队训练重构为上下文赌博机，由日志摘要器、规划器和代码器组成；可训练的 7B 规划器根据压缩日志生成完整攻击策略，冻结的代码器将其转换为可执行 Python 策略，并部署到实时 DRL 防御环境中。

Result: 实验显示，现有防御系统对自适应红队具有明显脆弱性。仅使用一个可训练的 7B 规划器，Trident 使蓝队防御性能相较静态红队基线平均下降 522%；同时发现了诱饵规避、自适应状态优先级调整等静态启发式方法无法产生的行为。该百分比表述较为异常，论文应进一步明确性能指标、计算方式和统计显著性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于 LLM 和 RLVR 的自适应红队能够有效揭示 DRL 防御系统在动态威胁下的泛化不足。Trident 为训练和评估自适应网络攻防智能体提供了统一框架，但其结论仍需通过更多环境、模型规模、攻击约束和现实部署条件进行验证。

Abstract: Autonomous cyber defense systems based on Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) have attracted significant research attention, yet remain evaluated almost exclusively against static, heuristic red agents, leaving their robustness against adaptive threats critically understudied. Meanwhile, recent advances in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) have improved LLM reasoning, but their integration into cybersecurity remains elusive due to the absence of suitable benchmark environments and interaction datasets. To bridge this gap, we introduce Trident, an agentic LLM red teaming framework comprising three components: a dynamic benchmark with isolated sandbox servers spanning CybORG CAGE 4 and CyberWheel, a dataset comprises over 13,000 high-fidelity red-blue interaction trajectories for RLVR, and a ``Code-as-Policy'' RLVR agentic architecture Trident Agentic). The latter reformulates red agent training as a contextual bandit via a tripartite Log Summarizer--Planner--Coder design, where a trainable Planner generates complete attack strategies from compressed execution logs, which a frozen Coder translates into executable Python policies deployed against live DRL defenders. Empirical evaluations reveal a fundamental brittleness in existing defenses: with a single trainable 7B planner, Trident reduces blue agent defensive performance by an average of 522% compared to static red agent baselines while autonomously discovering emergent behaviors such as decoy avoidance and adaptive state prioritization that static heuristics entirely fail to uncover.

</details>


### [90] [Combating Knowledge Corruption in Agent Systems: A Byzantine-Tolerant Secure Collaborative RAG Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04366)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Daqing He,Zijian Zhang,Ye Liu,Jiamou Liu,Zhirui Zeng,Zhan Qin,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Hongwei Yao,Jincheng An,Yong Liu,Yi Li,Qi Sun,Xiulei Liu,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SecureCollaRAG，一种面向检索增强生成系统的拜占庭容错协作框架，用于抵御通过投毒检索文档实施的知识篡改攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG虽能缓解大语言模型幻觉，但其外部知识库可能被恶意文档污染，进而操纵模型输出。现有机制难以在多源、非独立同分布数据环境下同时验证文档来源可信度并保持领域知识完整性。

Method: 框架采用多源知识验证机制，对多个来源的文档进行交叉验证；利用动态GNN生成与更新来源可信度评分，以识别可疑或隐蔽的污染信息；并结合拜占庭容错协作机制，使多个智能体在部分参与方不可信时仍能形成稳健的知识决策。

Result: 摘要声称，大规模实验和形式化分析表明，SecureCollaRAG能够在非IID数据分布下维持对知识篡改攻击的鲁棒性，并有效阻止隐蔽污染，同时保留关键领域知识的完整性。摘要未提供具体数据集、攻击比例、基线方法或量化性能指标。

Conclusion: 该工作将文档溯源验证、动态图神经网络可信度建模与拜占庭容错协作结合，为RAG知识库投毒防护提供了系统性方案。其主要价值在于多源协作环境下的鲁棒知识验证；但实际有效性仍需依赖完整论文中的威胁模型、评分机制、计算开销及实证指标进一步确认。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation systems partially address the hallucination issues in large language models, it also introduces new vulnerabilities to knowledge corruption attacks. Adversaries exploit these vulnerabilities by poisoning documents provided by RAG system to manipulate LLM outputs. To counter this threat, we propose SecureCollaRAG, a Byzantine-tolerant collaborative RAG framework leveraging Multi-source Knowledge Validation Mechanism. Our approach enables agent system to securely verify document provenance through dynamic GNN-based credibility scoring, effectively preventing stealthy knowledge corruption attacks while preserving essential domain knowledge integrity. Through extensive evaluations and formal analysis, we demonstrate that SecureCollaRAG maintains robustness against attackers under non-IID data distributions.

</details>


### [91] [Large Language Models and Social Media Information Integrity: Opportunities, Challenges, and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04375)
*Junjie Xiong,Zhengyuan Jiang,Xiaoran Xu,Chi Zhang,Changjia Zhu,Ning Wang,Mingkui Wei,Zhuo Lu,Yao Liu,Lingyao Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了2019—2024年间大语言模型对社交媒体信息完整性的双重影响：其既能强化恶意内容识别与防御，也可能被用于生成高可信度误导性内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在内容生成、语义理解和自动化交互中的能力提升，其对社交媒体信息生态的影响日益显著。研究旨在整合现有证据，明确其在信息完整性保护与风险扩散中的作用机制、技术局限和治理需求。

Method: 作者从多个数据库检索文献，初步筛选1048项研究，并对215篇代表性论文进行深入系统分析。分析覆盖误导性信息、虚假内容、自动化账号及隐私问题等维度，同时考察技术能力、伦理影响与隐私保护实践。

Result: 结果表明，大语言模型可提升恶意内容检测、语义分析和防御自动化水平；但其内容生成能力也会降低误导性内容的制作门槛并增强其可信度。现有研究仍在跨语言识别、实时监测及隐私保护型部署方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 大语言模型是社交媒体信息完整性治理中的双刃剑。未来研究应优先发展跨语言鲁棒检测、低延迟实时防护、隐私保护技术与可审计治理机制，并为平台、开发者和监管相关方提出协同风险缓解策略。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools that impact information integrity on social media platforms. This comprehensive review examines the dual role of LLMs in both facilitating and mitigating various information integrity challenges, including misinformation, disinformation, fake news, social bots, and privacy concerns. \textcolor{black}{We conduct a comprehensive review of the literature from 2019 to 2024, screening 1048 studies and performing an in-depth analysis of 215 representative papers. This systematic approach allows us to identify key patterns in how LLMs influence the information security in social media ecosystems.} Through a systematic analysis of papers from multiple databases, our findings reveal that while LLMs can enhance detection capabilities for malicious content and enable sophisticated defense mechanisms, they simultaneously pose risks by enabling the generation of highly convincing, deceptive content. We categorize and analyze the potential and challenges across different dimensions of information integrity, examining technical capabilities, ethical implications, and privacy concerns. The study demonstrates critical gaps in current approaches, particularly in cross-lingual detection, real-time monitoring, and privacy-preserving implementations. We conclude by proposing future research directions and recommendations for stakeholders to leverage LLMs while mitigating risks in social media information integrity.

</details>


### [92] [DeepInvert: Semi-Supervised Embedding Inversion Against Obfuscated Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04477)
*Zhicong Huang,Cheng Hong,Tao Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，面向云端语言模型的提示词表征混淆防御存在显著隐私漏洞；作者提出半监督嵌入反演攻击DeepInvert，可从扰动后的嵌入中恢复原始词元，并在多数评测场景中明显优于已有攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端语言模型服务常处理包含敏感信息的提示词。ObfusLM、SentinelLMs、TextObfuscator和DPNR等方法通过在传输前扰动或变换提示词表征来降低泄露风险，并以较低成本替代密码学方案。论文质疑这类防御的实际隐私保障，认为为了维持下游任务可用性而保留的语义信息可能被攻击者利用。

Method: 作者提出DeepInvert，一种半监督的嵌入反演方法。其首先利用带标签的影子数据进行监督训练，学习从混淆表征到原始词元的映射；随后针对无标签目标嵌入，引入无监督一致性目标，以挖掘扰动表征中仍然存在的语义结构。训练过程交替融合监督与无监督信号，并针对不同防御机制设计防御感知适配，从而覆盖基于编码器及自回归模型的多类架构。

Result: 在九种防御、五项任务和四种模型架构上的实验显示，DeepInvert在大多数防御设置下优于既有攻击。针对ObfusLM，其词元Top-1恢复率达到73.5%，显著高于此前最佳方法的26.2%。结果还表明存在明显的任务依赖性权衡：保留足够信息以支持任务效用的混淆方案，往往也保留了可被反演的结构；而更强的抗反演设置通常会导致效用下降。在较简单的分类任务中，部分差分隐私类防御可以同时维持一定效用与抗反演能力。

Conclusion: 论文认为现有表征混淆型提示词隐私防御的安全性被高估，不能仅以模型任务性能作为隐私保护有效性的依据。DeepInvert证明，无标签目标嵌入可显著增强反演攻击能力。作者据此呼吁重新评估该类防御，并在未来设计中系统衡量隐私、任务效用及跨模型和跨任务场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cloud-based language model services routinely process prompts containing sensitive information. Obfuscation-based defenses---including ObfusLM, SentinelLMs, TextObfuscator, and DPNR---mitigate this risk by transforming prompt representations before transmission, offering a lightweight alternative to cryptographic solutions. We show these defenses provide far less protection than previously believed.
  We present DeepInvert, a semi-supervised embedding inversion attack that recovers original tokens from obfuscated representations with higher accuracy than prior methods. The key insight is that unlabeled obfuscated embeddings retain exploitable semantic structure despite perturbation. DeepInvert combines supervised training on labeled shadow data with a novel unsupervised consistency objective over unlabeled target embeddings, alternating between the two via a mixed training pipeline. Defense-aware adaptations further extend the attack to diverse obfuscation mechanisms across encoder-based and autoregressive architectures.
  Experiments on nine defenses, five tasks, and four model architectures show that DeepInvert outperforms prior attacks on most defenses. Against ObfusLM, DeepInvert achieves 73.5\% top-1 token recovery versus 26.2\% for the previous best. Our results reveal a task-dependent tension: obfuscation schemes preserving enough signal for utility also retain sufficient structure for inversion, while schemes resisting inversion collapse utility. On simpler classification tasks, some DP-based defenses can maintain both. We call for a re-evaluation of this defense class.

</details>


### [93] [Checked-In Secret Detection: Strings Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04523)
*Zhengdong Huang,Kevin Li,Jinqiu Yang,Yepang Liu,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向源码硬编码密钥检测的字符串上下文提取方法StringGroup及检测工具Secretron。该方法仅聚焦候选密钥附近的字符串字面量，在显著缩短上下文的同时提升语义信号密度，并在准确性、混淆鲁棒性和跨语言泛化方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则表达式难以识别缺乏固定格式的密钥，通常存在较低的精确率和召回率。已有上下文感知方法虽可利用周边代码判断字符串用途，但容易依赖可被混淆的标识符，受训练语言分布不均影响，且长上下文包含大量无关代码，导致推理开销高、信噪比低。

Method: 作者观察到字符串字面量能够集中表达代码语义，并具有较强的语言独立性与抗标识符混淆能力。据此提出StringGroup：对既有候选密钥匹配模式进行简化修改，将上下文提取范围限定为候选项周围的字符串字面量。随后基于StringGroup构建Transformer检测工具Secretron，以提取后的字符串上下文作为主要语义输入进行密钥分类。

Result: 在SecretBench数据集上，Secretron取得98.74%的F1分数。StringGroup仅使用原始上下文的33.2%，但可保留超过80%的语义信息，同时提高密钥检测的信噪比。实验表明，该方法在代码混淆与跨语言场景下保持较强鲁棒性，并优于所比较的先进LLM基线。真实环境部署中，工具从26个应用中发现了48个此前未知的密钥。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献在于证明字符串字面量是一种高密度、低噪声且跨语言适用的密钥检测上下文。通过以字符串为中心的上下文压缩，StringGroup和Secretron在降低输入规模与推理成本的同时，实现了高精度、较强泛化能力和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Hardcoded secrets in source code pose critical security vulnerabilities which can be easily exploited by malicious adversaries. Existing regex-based detection approaches suffer from fundamental limitations, as secrets often lack identifiable patterns, resulting in poor precision and recall. Recent studies have explored context-aware detection methods, as surrounding code can reveal the purpose of candidate strings. However, these methods confront three key challenges: (1) obfuscation robustness where models over-rely on easily obfuscated identifiers, (2) cross-language generalization difficulties due to uneven training data distribution, and (3) lengthy and noisy context that introduces excessive irrelevant tokens and slows inference. We observe that strings serve as a critical information source for code semantics, offering superior contextual density, obfuscation robustness, and language independence. Based on this insight, we propose StringGroup, a novel context extraction algorithm that mines strings surrounding potential secrets. By introducing a relatively simple modification to existing patterns that narrows the analysis specifically to string literals, the method achieves significant gains. With only 33.2% of the original context, it preserves over 80% of semantic information and significantly improves the signal-to-noise ratio for secret detection. We further design a context-aware secret detection tool, Secretron, based on StringGroup methods and Transformer model. Evaluation on the SecretBench dataset demonstrates high accuracy with 98.74% F1-score and strong robustness under obfuscation and cross-language scenarios, outperforming state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines. We deploy our tool in real-world environments and successfully detect 48 previously unknown secret keys from 26 applications, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [94] [Breadcrumbing Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04565)
*Xuebin Li,Hanqing Zhao,Siyuan Liang,Kejiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,Dacheng Tao,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究基于LLM的搜索智能体在工具返回信息不可信条件下面临的提示注入与目标劫持风险，提出Authority-Chain Hijack（ACH）和Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution（TGSE）两种攻击方法，显示攻击者即使每次查询仅控制一个结果，也能通过跨步骤证据编排显著提升攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体安全研究主要关注单个静态网页中的注入内容，但真实智能体会执行多轮检索、追问和交叉验证，单页恶意信息通常可能被稀释或排除。论文指出，搜索结果和网页观测的传递通道本身是脆弱边界；若攻击者能够在智能体的多步检索轨迹中持续影响证据收集，就可能操控其最终结论。

Method: 论文采用受限的工具中介威胁模型：攻击者不能完全控制检索环境，但可在每次查询中附加一个受控结果。ACH通过将搜索结果操控和页面内容操控组织为连续、一致且表面上相互印证的“权威链”，诱导智能体在多轮推理中接受错误证据。TGSE则利用智能体执行轨迹自动评估和迭代攻击策略，以基于轨迹的策略优化替代人工重设计。

Result: 在完整SafeSearch测试划分上，ACH在所有基线中取得最高Overall ASR，ASR和MaxN ASR分别达到55.9%和83.3%。在留出评估中，TGSE最强的单一配置达到71.4% ASR和95.0% MaxN ASR，表明长轨迹、协同式证据操控能够显著增强针对搜索智能体的攻击效果。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是：搜索与网页观测接口不应被视为中性的信息通道，而应作为关键安全边界进行防护。仅依赖多源交叉验证不足以抵御跨查询协调的攻击；搜索智能体需要针对检索轨迹、来源独立性、证据链一致性及工具输出可信度设计动态防御机制。

Abstract: LLM-based search agents are widely used for information-seeking tasks, but their reliance on external tool returns introduces a critical security risk: web content retrieved during execution is untrusted, exposing agents to prompt injection and goal hijacking. Prior work on search-agent safety primarily focuses on static web-content injection, but modern agents issue follow-up queries and cross-check competing sources, so a single injected page is often diluted or rejected. We show that the channel delivering search and page observations is a fragile security boundary: beyond exposing the agent to a single poisoned page, a mediated search interface can repeatedly steer how the agent gathers evidence and forms its final answer. Under a constrained tool-intermediary threat model, appending only one controlled result per query can substantially increase attack success when the evidence is coordinated across the agent's trajectory. We study this setting with a strategy-driven long-horizon attack system and introduce Authority-Chain Hijack (ACH), an expert-refined strategy that turns isolated search-result and page-content manipulations into a coherent evidence chain across seemingly corroborating sources. ACH achieves the highest Overall ASR among all baselines, reaching 55.9% / 83.3% ASR / MaxN ASR on the full SafeSearch test split. We further introduce Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution (TGSE), which automatically improves attacker strategies from execution traces, replacing manual redesign with trace-driven refinement; its strongest single setting reaches 71.4% / 95.0% in held-out evaluation.

</details>


### [95] [Adaptive Intrusion Detection System using Transformer-Based Neural Networks and Continual Learning Approach with Adversarial Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04602)
*Azizi Ariffin,Afif Haris,Faiz Zaki,Hazim Hanif,Nor Badrul Anuar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出结合表格Transformer与类别均衡经验回放的持续学习入侵检测框架，在更贴近实际的类别-实例增量场景中实现近乎无遗忘的高准确率；但实验同时表明，回放缓冲区极易受到标签翻转与后门投毒影响，缓冲区完整性是部署前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定流量快照训练的入侵检测模型会因威胁分布变化而性能衰减。直接微调会产生灾难性遗忘，而从头重训成本高。现有回放方法通常假设正常流量仅出现在早期任务中，未反映真实网络中正常流量持续出现的情况，也忽略了回放缓冲区可能成为投毒入口的问题。

Method: 作者构建了由表格Transformer编码器和类别均衡经验回放缓冲区组成的自适应框架。在每次增量更新时均回放正常流量，以稳定正常与攻击类别之间的决策边界。论文提出类别-实例增量（CII）设定，使正常流量与新增攻击共同重现；并针对缓冲区评估两类威胁：显式标签翻转，以及隐蔽后门投毒。

Result: 在CICIDS2017数据集上，框架在传统类别增量设定下达到0.9994准确率，在CII设定下达到0.9989，遗忘程度可忽略，显著优于顺序微调（0.0052）、EWC（0.0324）、LwF（0.0699）和iCaRL（0.8770）。每轮经验中加入正常流量对避免遗忘至关重要。然而，缓冲区安全性较差：仅1%预算的标签翻转即可使准确率降至0.0053；后门投毒后，模型总体准确率仍为0.97，但触发样本攻击成功率达到95%，因而可规避仅监测整体性能的机制。

Conclusion: 类别均衡回放能够以较小缓冲预算获得接近联合训练的持续学习性能，且CII场景比传统类别增量设定更符合实际入侵检测需求。但回放机制同时扩大了供应链与数据完整性风险：总体准确率并不足以揭示后门问题，实际部署必须将回放缓冲区的来源验证、标签审计、投毒检测和访问保护视为严格的运行要求。

Abstract: Network intrusion detection systems (IDS) trained on fixed traffic snapshots decay silently after deployment as threat distributions shift. Fine-tuning models on new attacks triggers catastrophic forgetting, while retraining from scratch is computationally infeasible. Replay-based continual learning counters this, but existing methods unrealistically confine benign traffic to a single early task and ignore the replay buffer as a potential attack surface. To address this, we present an adaptive IDS framework coupling a tabular transformer encoder with a class balanced experience replay buffer that replays benign traffic at every update to stabilize decision boundaries. We introduce the class-instance incremental (CII) scenario where benign flows reappear alongside new attacks as a more faithful stress test, and probe the buffer with overt label flipping and stealthy backdoor poisoning attacks. On the CICIDS2017 benchmark, our framework achieved 0.9994 accuracy under the traditional class incremental setup and 0.9989 under CII, with negligible forgetting, drastically outperforming sequential fine-tuning (0.0052), EWC (0.0324), LwF (0.0699), and iCaRL (0.8770) baselines. While injecting benign traffic into every experience proves essential for preventing forgetting, the replay buffer introduces critical vulnerabilities. Label-flipping collapses the model entirely (0.0053 accuracy at a 1% budget), and the backdoor maintains 0.97 overall accuracy while driving the attack success rate on trigger flows to 95%, evading standard monitoring. Ultimately, while a modest replay budget recovers near-joint-training performance, ensuring buffer integrity emerges as a strict operational requirement.

</details>


### [96] [Blockchain Empowered Trustworthy Agent Networks: Foundations, Taxonomy, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04626)
*Liehuang Zhu,Yuhang Li,Tianxing Wang,Zhihao Chen,Ke Li,Hongyi Liu,Yajie Wang,Lei Xu,Peng Jiang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该文是一篇综述与教程型论文，系统讨论从传统多智能体系统向开放智能体网络演进过程中出现的网络级信任问题，并评估区块链作为共享信任层对身份、授权、溯源、协作审计、激励与结算的支撑作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的智能体正从封闭环境中的独立任务执行者，发展为可跨平台通信、委托任务、调用工具和访问外部知识的自治网络参与者。由于不同主体拥有的异构智能体之间缺少统一的身份、权限、审计、声誉和结算基础设施，单智能体安全措施及传统封闭式多智能体协调机制难以解决跨组织、跨平台交互中的信任风险。

Method: 论文回顾1980—2026年的相关研究，覆盖经典多智能体系统、LLM智能体、智能体互操作协议、Internet-of-Agents基础设施及区块链信任机制。作者将信任边界扩展过程归纳为从个体执行到跨智能体、跨平台和跨组织协作，并提出五维信任分类法：实体与能力信任、授权与委托信任、信息与来源信任、协调与群体鲁棒性信任、问责与结算信任。随后建立智能体网络风险、信任需求与区块链机制之间的映射关系。

Result: 文章指出，开放智能体网络面临的是网络层面的信任危机，其核心并非单一智能体是否安全，而是多方主体间身份真实性、能力声明、权限传递、信息来源、协作过程、责任归属及价值交换是否可验证。区块链可提供共享身份、可验证授权、防篡改来源记录、可审计协作、激励对齐及价值结算等能力，从而为开放智能体网络构建公共信任基础设施。

Conclusion: 区块链适合被定位为开放智能体网络的共享信任层，而非替代智能体自身安全、语义正确性验证、隐私保护或鲁棒推理能力的万能方案。论文的主要贡献在于提出面向开放智能体网络的系统化信任分类框架，并阐明区块链机制在不同信任维度中的适用角色与边界。

Abstract: AI agents are evolving from isolated task executors into networked autonomous entities that can communicate, delegate tasks, invoke tools, access external knowledge, and participate in cross-platform service and economic workflows. This evolution gives rise to open agent networks, where heterogeneous agents owned by different stakeholders interact without naturally shared infrastructures for identity, authorization, auditability, reputation, or settlement. This survey and tutorial article reviews the literature over the period 1980--2026 on the evolution from classical multi-agent systems to open agent networks, with a particular focus on LLM-based autonomous agents, agent interoperability protocols, Internet-of-Agents infrastructures, and blockchain-enabled trust mechanisms. We first review this evolution and show how the trust boundary expands from individual execution to cross-agent, cross-platform, and cross-organizational interaction. We then identify a network-level trust crisis that cannot be fully addressed by single-agent safety mechanisms or closed multi-agent coordination techniques, and develop a five-dimensional taxonomy covering entity and capability trust, authorization and delegation trust, information and provenance trust, coordination and group-robustness trust, and accountability and settlement trust. Based on this taxonomy, we examine how blockchain can provide shared identity, verifiable authorization, tamper-evident provenance, auditable collaboration, incentive alignment, and value settlement for trustworthy agent networks. We further synthesize the mapping between agent-network risks, trust requirements, and blockchain-enabled mechanisms, and clarify the role of blockchain as a shared trust layer rather than a replacement for agent security, semantic verification, privacy protection, or robust reasoning.

</details>


### [97] [MOAT: Model-Agnostic Randomized Transformations for preventing Efficiency Degradation Attacks on ViTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04680)
*Anadi Goyal,Nandish Chattopadhyay,Chandan Karfa,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出MOAT，一种面向高效视觉Transformer的模型无关输入预处理防御方案，用于缓解针对令牌剪枝机制的对抗性效率攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 令牌剪枝可降低视觉Transformer在资源受限环境中的计算成本，但攻击者可通过特定扰动削弱剪枝带来的效率收益，导致推理计算量显著上升。

Method: MOAT在模型输入端组合多种输入变换，不修改视觉Transformer架构、既有令牌剪枝模块或训练流程，因此可作为通用的前处理防御管线部署于不同高效ViT实现中。

Result: 实验表明，在所评估的全部ViT模型上，MOAT可将对抗攻击造成的GFLOPs劣化控制在相对于未受攻击原始模型3.4%以内。

Conclusion: MOAT以模型无关、无需改造内部结构的方式，提升了令牌剪枝ViT面对效率攻击时的计算稳定性，并在保持高效部署兼容性的同时有效限制额外计算开销。

Abstract: To adopt the Vision Transformers (ViTs) in resource-constrained environment, token pruning is widely used to reduce computational cost without impacting accuracy. However, adversaries have developed targeted attacks against said token pruning techniques to undermine such attempts to make ViTs efficient. In this paper, we propose MOAT, a model-agnostic pre-processing defense pipeline that applies a combination of input transformations to protect efficient ViT implementations against adversarial efficiency attacks. MOAT operates directly on the input without requiring modifications to the model architecture or token pruning mechanism. Experimental results demonstrate that, across all evaluated ViT models, MOAT limits GFLOPs degradation under adversarial attacks to within 3.4% of the original unattacked model.

</details>


### [98] [LoginTrap: Uncovering Task-Agnostic Phishing-Style Indirect Prompt Injection Attacks against LLM-based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04741)
*Longtao Guo,Zelin Zhang,Kaifeng Huang,Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出LoginTrap，一种面向基于大语言模型的网页智能体的任务无关登录诱导攻击。攻击者通过操控网页上下文，使智能体将登录误认为完成当前任务的必要步骤，并引导其进入受控登录页面，从而形成凭据或隐私数据泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页智能体能够代替用户浏览网页和执行操作，而登录环节同时涉及身份认证、凭据及敏感信息，是关键安全边界。既有研究主要关注恶意网页内容对智能体动作的操控，但尚未充分验证攻击者能否在不了解用户任务和智能体内部实现的情况下，诱导智能体发起登录并造成端到端隐私泄露。

Method: 作者构建LoginTrap威胁模型：攻击者以黑盒方式控制网页内容及被诱导的登录流程，但未知用户具体任务和智能体内部机制。该方法通过受模糊测试启发的过程生成与当前页面相关的间接提示注入内容，将登录包装为推进任务的合理前提，并将智能体引导至攻击者控制的登录页面。实验覆盖真实网页智能体执行场景、不同大语言模型骨干、不同智能体架构及若干防御设置。

Result: 实验显示，LoginTrap在不同大语言模型骨干上取得平均86%的端到端攻击成功率；其效果在多种智能体架构和防御条件下仍具有较强稳定性。这表明登录诱导不是局部提示注入问题，而是能够跨模型与系统设计复现的认证边界风险。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了网页智能体中的登录诱导会系统性削弱认证边界：即使攻击者不了解任务和系统内部细节，也可能借助页面上下文促使智能体进入不可信登录流程。未来防御应将认证状态、登录来源可信性、跨站跳转验证、用户显式确认和凭据输入隔离纳入智能体安全设计，而不能仅依赖通用提示注入防护。

Abstract: LLM-based web agents automate user tasks by observing webpages and executing browser actions on behalf of users. As these agents operate on real web services, login becomes a sensitive authentication boundary because it involves credentials and sensitive information. Existing work shows that malicious webpage content can manipulate web agent actions, but it has not fully examined whether such content can induce login and cause end-to-end private data leakage. We study this attack surface and present LoginTrap, a task-agnostic login-inducing attack against LLM-based web agents. LoginTrap assumes a black box attacker that controls the webpage context and the induced login flow without knowing the user task or web agent internals. Under this threat model, LoginTrap uses webpage context to generate page-specific indirect injections through a fuzzing-inspired process, making login appear as a plausible prerequisite for continuing the task and guiding the agent to a controlled login page. We conduct a comprehensive analysis of LoginTrap across realistic web agent executions. The results show that LoginTrap reaches 86\% average end-to-end attack success across LLM backbones and remains effective across agent architectures and defenses. These findings identify login inducement as a systematic authentication boundary risk and motivate further research on authentication-aware defenses for web agents.

</details>


### [99] [Web Cache Overflow: Exploiting Imprecise Keys for Cache Degradation and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04744)
*Matteo Golinelli,Kaan Onarlioglu,Bruno Crispo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究了Web缓存中因缓存键设计不精确而产生的冗余缓存条目问题，并指出该问题可降低缓存命中率、增加源站负载，进而形成依赖缓存驱逐的服务可用性风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web缓存依赖缓存键识别可复用对象，但运营者常将实际上不影响响应内容的请求字段纳入缓存键。攻击者可利用这些冗余字段为同一对象构造大量不同键值的请求，造成对象重复缓存，因此需要评估其对缓存效率与源站负载的安全影响。

Method: 作者在五种独立缓存代理上复现实验，持续发送指向同一资源但具有不同缓存键的请求，以制造冗余副本；随后测量缓存退化现象，并分析相关参数对攻击成本和缓存命中率的影响。

Result: 实验表明，不必要地纳入缓存键的请求元素能够导致大量冗余缓存条目。在易受影响的部署中，持续生成这些条目会挤占缓存容量、降低命中率、提高回源请求比例和源站负载；攻击效果受缓存配置及请求参数空间等因素影响。

Conclusion: 精确定义缓存键是缓解该问题最直接且关键的措施。缓存键应仅包含确实会改变响应表示的请求字段；将最小化、规范化和审计缓存键设计作为安全最佳实践，有助于避免冗余条目导致的缓存效率下降与服务可用性风险。

Abstract: Web caches support the scalability needs of contemporary web applications by storing frequently accessed objects closer to clients. Web caches are conceptually associative arrays, tracking stored objects using cache keys consisting of HTTP request fields. However, these cache keys are often imprecisely defined by website operators. This allows clients to craft a multitude of requests that target the same object, but map to different cache keys.
  In this work, we show that request elements included unnecessarily in cache keys can be abused to create redundant cache entries. In susceptible deployments, sustained generation of such redundant copies reduces cache effectiveness and increases origin load, facilitating eviction-dependent attacks. Our experiments reproduce cache degradation across five stand-alone caching proxies and characterize how these parameters affect attacker cost and cache hit rate, potentially resulting in denial-of-service attacks. We conclude that precise cache-key design is the most direct mitigation against this abuse vector and should be recognized as a security best practice.

</details>


### [100] ["Allow" to Achieve, Over-Privileged Inadvertently: The Unintended Cost of Task-Completion-Driven Pop-up Decisions in Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04755)
*Dongsheng Chen,Yuxuan Li,Guanhua Chen,Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao,Lei Ma,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估移动端GUI智能体在权限弹窗中的“权限素养”：能否仅授予完成当前任务所必需的权限。结果显示，前沿多模态模型会受到应用身份与任务上下文的显著影响，存在不必要授权及漏授权风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体在真实任务中频繁处理系统权限请求，但既有研究较少检验其是否能依据任务必要性和隐私风险作出最小权限决策。该研究旨在量化这一安全能力，并识别影响授权判断的关键偏差。

Method: 作者构建了按任务相关性和隐私风险划分的四级权限框架，并由三名GUI智能体安全专家验证场景。研究将Android风格权限弹窗注入真实GUI任务，向四个前沿多模态大模型提供同步标注截图和UI树信息。进一步通过控制变量实验，分别改变任务上下文与智能体可见的请求应用身份，以分析授权决策的因果影响；同时评估提示词干预的效果。

Result: 在相同日历任务中，仅将权限请求者从Calendar替换为PiMusic，就使授权次数从26/32降至0/32，表明模型存在强烈但依赖任务条件的“应用信任偏差”。在保持弹窗不变而改变任务上下文时，授权决定也显著变化，体现出“任务先验覆盖”现象。提示词可减少部分非必要授权，但不同模型间效果不稳定，并可能同时压制本应批准的合法权限。

Conclusion: 当前GUI智能体的权限决策并不稳定地遵循最小权限原则，而会受请求应用身份和任务语境的系统性影响。论文建议将任务执行与权限授权解耦，通过独立的授权机制、显式任务—权限匹配及风险约束，提升移动智能体的权限安全性。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents routinely encounter system permission dialogs during task execution, yet their ability to grant only permissions that are necessary for the delegated task remains largely unexamined. We present a systematic study of this capability, which we term Permission Literacy. We construct a four-level permission framework based on task relevance and privacy risk and validate the evaluated scenarios with three independent experts in GUI-agent safety. We inject Android-style permission popups into real GUI tasks and evaluate four frontier multimodal large language models using synchronized annotated screenshots and UI-tree hierarchies, making the requester, permission, justification, and available actions accessible to the agent. Beyond the main study, we conduct controlled interventions that separately vary task context and agent-visible requester identity. Under the same Calendar task, changing only the requester from Calendar to PiMusic reduces grants from 26/32 to 0/32, revealing a strong but task-conditioned App-Trust Bias. Holding a popup fixed while changing task context also substantially changes authorization decisions, revealing a systematic Task-Prior Override. Prompt interventions can reduce unnecessary grants, but their effectiveness is inconsistent across models and may come at the cost of suppressing legitimate grants. These results suggest that separating task execution from permission authorization is a promising design direction for future work.

</details>


### [101] [PURPOSE: Poisoning Conflict Resolution in RAG via Proxy-Fact-Grounded Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04756)
*Zijian Wang,Yubo Zhu,Muzhi Dong,Yanjun Lou,Yisheng Li,ZiLiang Zhang,Wei Tong,Yuan Zhang,Jingyu Hua,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出PURPOSE，一种面向检索增强生成（RAG）系统的严格黑盒知识投毒方法。其核心是以“看似一致的知识更新”替代直接矛盾的错误断言，从而绕过检索后冲突消解机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常依赖冲突消解模块，在检索内容存在噪声或相互矛盾时选择可信信息。既有黑盒投毒方法直接植入与既定事实冲突的目标答案，容易被该模块识别和过滤。因此，作者研究冲突消解机制在面对非直接矛盾、但具有误导性的注入内容时的鲁棒性。

Method: PURPOSE首先提取与查询相关的事实，以近似冲突消解器可能使用的参考知识；随后将一个“枢纽事件”建立在这些事实之上，使注入内容表面上可被已有事实支持、与可验证信息保持一致，同时引导生成模型输出攻击者设定的目标答案。该方法不依赖目标模型内部信息，属于严格黑盒设定。

Result: 在3个问答基准、5个生成器和3种冲突消解方法构成的45组实验中，PURPOSE在35组设置下取得最高攻击成功率。相较最强既有方法，其平均攻击成功率提高9.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，RAG中的冲突消解并不能充分防御以非矛盾形式呈现的知识投毒。将错误引导包装为与已有事实相容的“更新”是更有效的攻击模式；这提示RAG防护应不仅检测显式事实冲突，还需评估事件链、因果关联和语义一致性下的隐蔽误导风险。

Abstract: In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), post-retrieval conflict resolution arbitrates among noisy or contradictory retrieved passages. However, the robustness of this safeguard against knowledge poisoning has not been adequately studied. Existing black-box poisoning methods all assert the target answer in frontal contradiction with what the resolver treats as settled, the very signal these methods are built to detect. We propose PURPOSE, a strict black-box poisoning attack that reframes the injection as an update that minimizes conflict, rather than as a counter-claim. PURPOSE extracts query-related facts approximating the resolver's possible reference, then grounds a pivot event in them to keep the injection consistent with what the resolver might verify while steering the generator toward the target answer. Across three QA benchmarks, five generators, and three conflict-resolution methods, PURPOSE attains the highest attack success rate (ASR) in 35 of 45 settings and exceeds the strongest prior attack with +9.7 mean ASR points. These results show that our poisoning method is effective against conflict resolution in RAG and identify non-contradicting injection as a practical mode to enhance poisoning attack.

</details>


### [102] [Hidden Ciphers and Where to Find Them: Static Discovery and Assessment of Cryptographic Assets in Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04857)
*Christian Näther,Eduard Hirsch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向软件系统的静态密码资产发现方法，通过统一分类、可扩展规则库和扫描器生成面向CBOM的结果，以提升密码治理与后量子迁移规划的可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织通常无法系统掌握源代码、配置、依赖项和密码文件中实际部署的密码机制，导致密码治理、风险识别及后量子迁移评估困难。现有实践缺乏统一的资产分类和可复用的发现规则。

Method: 作者定义Crypto-Material、Crypto-Artifacts和Crypto-Invocations三类对象，据此构建与具体扫描器解耦、可扩展的规则仓库，并实现静态扫描器。该工具扫描软件项目中的相关文件和调用信息，识别密码资产、标注潜在弱点与漏洞，并输出CBOM导向的资产清单。评估包括具有已知真值的合成基准，以及由十个已部署服务构成的真实基础设施。

Result: 在合成基准上，密码资产发现的F1分数为0.75；对预期弱点和漏洞的标注正确率为91%。在真实环境中，工具于六分钟内处理57,610个文件，发现370项密码资产、6项关联CVE的漏洞，以及52项后量子迁移候选项。真实环境的覆盖率依据人工编制的参考列表评估，而非完整真值集，因此覆盖性结论存在一定限制。

Conclusion: 分类驱动的静态分析能够以可操作的成本提供密码资产透明度，并支持密码治理、风险排查和后量子迁移规划。论文的主要价值在于建立可复用的密码资产分类与规则框架；但资产发现准确率仍有提升空间，且真实场景评估缺少穷尽性参考标准，未来应扩展规则覆盖范围并通过更完整的标注数据验证召回率。

Abstract: Modern software systems rely on cryptography for data protection, authentication, and trust establishment, yet organizations often lack a structured view of the cryptography deployed across source code, configuration, dependencies, and cryptographic files. This lack of visibility complicates security governance and post-quantum migration planning. This paper presents a static approach for discovering and assessing cryptographic assets in software systems. We introduce a classification of Crypto-Material, Crypto-Artifacts, and Crypto-Invocations, derive an extensible scanner-independent rule repository from it, and implement a static scanner that applies these rules to produce CBOM-oriented output. We evaluate the approach on a synthetic benchmark with known ground truth and on a real-world infrastructure of ten deployed services. The scanner achieves an F1 score of 0.75 for asset discovery and correctly annotates 91% of expected weaknesses and vulnerabilities. In the realworld setting, it processes 57 610 files in under six minutes and discovers 370 cryptographic assets, including six CVE-linked vulnerabilities and 52 post-quantum migration candidates. Real-world coverage is assessed against a manually compiled reference list rather than an exhaustive one. These results show that classification-driven static discovery can provide practical cryptographic transparency for governance and post-quantum migration planning.

</details>


### [103] [HoRFFI: High-Openness RF Fingerprint Identification with a Similarity-Enhanced Variational Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04881)
*Shuiguang Zeng,Yuxiang Shen,Yuanyu Zhang,Yulong Shen,Zhiyuan Tan,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出HoRFFI框架，面向仅有少量已标注设备类别训练数据的高开放度射频指纹识别场景，实现可扩展设备识别与未知设备拒识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放集识别方法通常依赖大量且多样化的已知设备类别学习特征空间，难以适用于实际部署中训练设备数量有限、但持续有新授权设备加入及未知设备出现的场景。

Method: HoRFFI采用基于相似性增强变分信息瓶颈（SVIB）的监督机制。该机制通过特征空间增强与聚类挖掘样本间相似性，将其作为补充监督信号，以正则化嵌入空间、降低编码器对训练类别多样性的依赖，并提升特征表示的迁移能力。

Result: 在公开LoRa和Wi-Fi数据集上，相对最优基线，HoRFFI的新增类别准确率分别绝对提升0.112和0.288；对应的未知类别区分AUC分别绝对提升0.029和0.060。

Conclusion: HoRFFI表明，通过利用样本相似性构造额外监督，即使训练阶段仅使用少量设备类别，也能学习更具泛化性的射频指纹嵌入表示，并显著改善新设备识别与未知设备拒识性能。

Abstract: Radio frequency fingerprint identification (RFFI) is a promising technique for wireless device authentication. However, practical RFFI systems must enroll newly authorized devices while rejecting previously unseen ones, even when the feature extractor is trained on only a few labeled base-device classes, giving rise to a high-openness RFFI problem. Existing open-set recognition methods typically rely on feature spaces learned from a large and diverse set of known-device classes, limiting their applicability in practical scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose HoRFFI, a high-openness RFFI framework that supports scalable device identification and unknown-device rejection using only a small number of labeled training devices. HoRFFI employs a similarity-enhanced variational information bottleneck (SVIB)-based supervision mechanism, which reduces the encoder's dependence on training-class diversity and learns a more transferable embedding space. This supervision mechanism uses feature-space augmentation and clustering to derive inter-sample similarity information, which provides supplementary supervision for regularizing the embedding space. Experiments on public LoRa and Wi-Fi datasets show that HoRFFI achieves absolute improvements of \(0.112\) and \(0.288\) in novel-class accuracy, respectively, and corresponding absolute AUC improvements of \(0.029\) and \(0.060\) over the best-performing baselines.

</details>


### [104] [When Does Latent Communication Pay? A Causal Audit of Relayed KV Caches in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04893)
*Jiaming Cheng,Subhransu Das,Rajiv Ramnath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文对多智能体大语言模型中“通过转发KV缓存交换潜在思维”的说法进行因果审计。核心发现是：KV缓存带来性能提升，并不等于接收方利用了与当前样本相匹配的发送方信息；只有错配缓存（mismatched-example cache）对照才能检验是否存在真正的样本特异性传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统常将KV缓存通信的基准性能增益归因于“潜在思维”传输，但这一归因缺少对照：即使任意缓存、随机统计特征或通信结构本身都可能改善结果，也会产生性能提升。作者因此区分“缓存是否有用”与“特定样本的缓存是否被有效传递”两个问题，并要求后者接受因果验证。

Method: 作者在已发布系统中，将正常转发的KV缓存分别替换为：来自其他样本的错配缓存、全零缓存，以及矩匹配随机缓存。实验按接收方是否需要发送方私有信息划分为两类：若需要私有信息，样本配对应具有决定性作用；若不需要，则通过预注册的五随机种子等效性检验，判断匹配与错配缓存的差异是否落在预设容忍界限内。统计上采用Holm校正的双单侧检验（TOST），并覆盖GSM8K、ARC-Challenge、MedQA、文档问答任务、多个模型家族、检查点和Qwen3参数规模。

Result: 在接收方必须依赖发送方私有信息的设定下，匹配缓存的表现达到100%，而与答案无关的转发仅为23%至25%，这一结果在三个模型家族、五个检查点及文档问答任务中复现，说明存在强样本特异性信息转移。然而，在接收方不需要发送方私有信息时，匹配与错配缓存通常在2.8个百分点等效界限内，表明未检测到显著的样本特异性优势；一个实验单元虽有小优势，但仍位于该界限内。作者还展示：某自然实验中，完全移除缓存使性能下降14.7点，但替换为错配缓存仅下降0.4点，说明“大缓存效应”不等于“配对效应”。不同通信方案差异明显：LatentMAS原生中继接近满分，KVComm仅部分有效，而C2C公开投影器未检测到样本特异性传输。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是提出并公开“错配缓存审计”作为验证潜在通信主张的必要因果工具。仅报告KV缓存通信相对于无通信的基准增益，不能证明系统传递了与具体输入实例相关的潜在推理信息。可信证据应显示：在理论上需要发送方私有信息时，正确配对缓存显著优于错配缓存；在不需要该信息时，两者则应接近等效。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems relay key--value caches instead of text and credit their gains to exchanged ``latent thoughts''. That credit is a claim about \emph{which} example's cache is relayed, not merely that one is. We audit it causally in released systems. The cache is replaced with deranged (mismatched-example), zeroed, and moment-matched random counterparts, under two regimes defined by whether the receiver needs the sender's private information. Where it does, the battery reads ceiling: 100\% against 23--25\% for answer-irrelevant relays on the primary backbone, a contrast replicated across three families, five checkpoints, and a prose document-QA surface. Where it does not, a pre-registered five-seed protocol establishes equivalence within 2.8 points, a margin anchored to the audited system's reported gain, under Holm-corrected TOST on GSM8K and ARC-Challenge across three Qwen3 scales and on MedQA at 8B (one cell shows a small detected advantage inside the margin); a second family shows no detected advantage. A large cache effect need not be a pairing effect. In one natural cell, zeroing the relay costs 14.7 points; a mismatched cache, 0.4. Nor is need sufficient: under the same test, delivered channels span ceiling (LatentMAS's native relay), partial (KVComm's layer subset), and no detected example-specific transfer (C2C's released projector). Benchmark deltas do not by themselves establish latent-thought transmission; establishing it takes a mismatched-cache audit, which we release.

</details>


### [105] [LLM-Assisted Detection and Repair of Hardware Security Vulnerabilities in Verilog Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04907)
*Ethen Santana,Gabriel Gyaase,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出利用大语言模型直接分析Verilog硬件设计，以自动识别与CWE框架对应的潜在硬件安全弱点，并在单模块设计数据集上进行迭代评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件漏洞一旦随芯片制造固化，通常难以通过后续更新修复，因此需要在设计阶段尽早发现访问控制缺陷、敏感信息暴露和非预期权限提升等安全问题。传统分析流程的自动化与扩展性有限，促使作者探索LLM辅助检测方法。

Method: 作者构建了一种基于LLM的分析方法，将Verilog硬件设计作为输入，识别其中可能对应的硬件CWE弱点；随后在由单模块Verilog设计组成的数据集上迭代测试，以评估该方法的漏洞检测能力。

Result: 摘要表明，该方法展示了LLM用于发现硬件安全弱点的可行性，并能够为传统硬件安全分析提供自动化、可扩展的辅助能力。但摘要未给出数据集规模、具体提示或模型配置、检测准确率、召回率、误报率及与基线方法的定量比较。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献是将LLM应用于Verilog级硬件CWE识别，为设计早期的安全审查提供辅助。其结论目前主要体现为潜力验证；要证明实际工程价值，还需补充严格的量化评估、跨模块与系统级验证、对误报漏报的分析，以及与规则检查、形式验证和传统静态分析工具的比较。

Abstract: Hardware designs, like software, are susceptible to bugs that can introduce security vulnerabilities and create opportunities for malicious exploitation. Unlike software vulnerabilities, however, hardware flaws become permanently embedded in silicon after fabrication, making them difficult or impossible to patch. Many of these weaknesses are categorized under the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) framework and include improper access control, exposure of sensitive information, and unintended privilege escalation. To improve the detection of such vulnerabilities, we propose a methodology that leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) to identify potential hardware CWEs directly from hardware designs in Verilog. The proposed approach is evaluated iteratively on a dataset of single-module Verilog designs to assess its effectiveness in detecting hardware security weaknesses. Our results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to augment traditional hardware security analysis by providing automated, scalable assistance for identifying security vulnerabilities during the hardware design process.

</details>


### [106] [Toward Practical Decentralized Proof-of-Location via Physical Witnessing Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04957)
*Tamor Tomson,Eduardo Brito,Amnir Hadachi,Ulrich Norbisrath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出并实现了一套低成本、去中心化的物理位置证明原型，将抽象的地点在场声明转化为可由独立验证者事后检查的防篡改证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字服务通常依赖设备或用户自行上报的位置数据，该数据易被伪造且难以追溯验证。论文旨在弥合理论化去中心化位置证明架构与真实物理部署之间的差距。

Method: 作者构建了一个室内见证区域：固定见证设备测量证明者的在场状态，通过本地网状网络交换声明，并将结果写入防篡改账本。为适应真实硬件、无线通信和时间同步约束，系统采用见证者主动测距、跨见证者一致性检查及与新鲜度绑定的抗重放机制。

Result: 受控室内实验表明，该原型能够生成准确且低时延的位置证明对象，并可识别模拟重放和恶意测距行为。系统验证了低成本硬件实现去中心化位置证明的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可复用的实验基线，证明去中心化位置证据可在真实无线、网络与时序条件下运行。后续仍需解决测距校准、验证者独立性、无线信号完整性及大规模部署等问题。

Abstract: Digital services increasingly rely on claims that a person, device, or asset was in a specific place at a specific time. Today, those claims often depend on self-reported location data, which is easy to falsify and difficult to verify after the fact. Proof-of-Location (PoL) systems address this gap by turning presence claims into evidence that an independent verifier can later inspect. This paper builds upon recent theoretical work on decentralized PoL architectures and demonstrates how they can move from emulation to a physical prototype built with low-cost hardware. We implement a witnessing zone in which fixed nearby devices measure a prover's presence, exchange claims over a local mesh, and record them in a tamper-evident ledger. Building the prototype required adapting the abstract protocol to physical constraints through witness-initiated ranging, cross-witness consistency checks, and freshness binding against replay. Our controlled indoor evaluation shows that the system can produce accurate, low-latency proof objects while detecting simulated replay and malicious-ranging attacks. The result is a reusable experimental baseline for next-generation digital trust infrastructure, that exposes the remaining calibration, verifier-independence, radio-integrity, and scaling requirements for decentralized location evidence, where physical presence claims can be independently checked under real radio, networking, and timing conditions.

</details>


### [107] [When Do PEFT Adaptations Leak Structure? Measuring Black-Box Structural Bounds in Public-Base Model Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05036)
*Zhongjiang Yao,Shuangshuang Liang,Chun Yang,LiWei Chen,Gang Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出VectorHijack-SR，用于评估公开基座模型与私有PEFT适配器共存时的结构信息泄露。方法可从受害服务输出与本地基座模型输出的残差中推断PEFT家族、层位置、粗粒度秩及部分版本信息；但其对实际适配器恢复和自由生成攻击的增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当服务公开基础模型、但将LoRA等参数高效微调适配器私有化部署时，攻击者可在本地运行已知基座模型，并通过比较本地输出与服务输出获得残差信号。现有评估往往将闭集分类准确率直接等同于适配器可恢复性，忽略了元数据可见性、开放集泛化能力与实际攻击可利用性之间的差异。

Method: VectorHijack-SR首先构造同一查询下服务模型与公开基座模型输出的配对残差；随后提取幅值、排序、熵、置信边际、输出长度、模板、层局部性和谱统计等查询级特征，并汇聚为服务级表示。作者使用按服务划分的数据集训练结构分类器，避免同一服务样本同时出现在训练和测试中；又通过交叉拟合的层级拒识器，判断未知服务是否处于已校准的LoRA结构流形之外。实验还分别评估PEFT家族识别、秩推断、开放集拒识、精确版本关联，以及这些信息对两阶段恢复、PEFT选择和自由生成攻击的实际帮助。

Result: PEFT家族泄露在多个模型与任务上高于均匀随机水平：BERT/MNLI为8/12，RoBERTa/MNLI为21/24，DeBERTa-v3在MNLI和AG News上分别为12/18与15/18。秩推断具有明显任务依赖性：BERT/MNLI和DeBERTa/AG News达到8/9，而DeBERTa/MNLI仅为4/9，经多重校正后与随机表现相容。十随机种子BERT开放集实验中，拒识器的汇总AUROC为0.804，95%置信区间为[0.660, 0.927]，已知类准确率为0.956；但对结构接近的DoRA及LoRA加分类头变体仍较困难。对5个留出的LoRA-r64服务，精确版本关联AUC达到0.940。与此同时，结构泄露未直接转化为稳定攻击优势：两阶段恢复未带来公平查询预算下的节省，基于后验选择的PEFT性能低于先蒸馏再转换PEFT的方案，数值为0.356对0.517，自由运行生成接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 论文表明，在已知基座模型且服务暴露丰富输出的条件下，私有PEFT适配器会泄露可操作的结构与版本线索，尤其是家族、部分秩信息和精确版本关联。然而，闭集识别或高置信度结构分类不能证明适配器可被普遍恢复。风险评估应区分结构可识别性、开放集有效性和端到端攻击收益，并重点关注输出接口丰富度及与已知PEFT结构的接近程度。

Abstract: Services increasingly deploy public foundation models with private parameter-efficient adaptations, creating a differential information leakage risk when auditors or adversaries can execute the public base model locally and observe victim outputs. We present VectorHijack-SR, a measurement methodology that converts paired victim/base residuals into calibrated structural bounds over PEFT family, layer locality, and coarse rank, while separating metadata visibility from open-world validity and operational exploitability. Our estimator aggregates query-level magnitude, ranking, entropy, margin, length, template, locality, and spectral statistics into service-level representations. A service-disjoint classifier quantifies structural evidence, and a cross-fitted hierarchical rejector evaluates whether a victim lies outside the calibrated LoRA manifold. Across classification backbones, family leakage exceeds uniform chance on BERT/MNLI (8/12), RoBERTa/MNLI (21/24), and DeBERTa-v3 on MNLI (12/18) and AG News (15/18). Rank inference is task dependent: BERT/MNLI and DeBERTa/AG News reach 8/9, whereas DeBERTa/MNLI achieves 4/9 and is statistically compatible with chance after correction. On a ten-seed BERT open-set grid, the rejector achieves pooled AUROC 0.804 (95% CI [0.660, 0.927]) and known accuracy 0.956, but remains limited on structurally close DoRA and LoRA+head variants. Exact-version linkage on five held-out LoRA-r64 services reaches AUC 0.940. Experiments reveal a visibility-exploitability gap: two-stage recovery provides no fair-budget query savings, posterior-selected PEFT underperforms distill-then-convert PEFT (0.356 vs. 0.517), and free-running generation remains near chance. These results show that known-base, rich-output PEFT services can leak actionable structural and version information, while closed-set confidence alone does not establish universal adapter recovery.

</details>


### [108] [Private Direct Preference Optimization for LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05040)
*Yangfan Jiang,Fei Wei,Ergute Bao,Xiaokui Xiao,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出PrivDPO，一种面向直接偏好优化（DPO）的专用隐私保护方法。其核心是在仅保护候选回答相对偏好标签的“偏好隐私”定义下，只对承载偏好信息的一维梯度方向注入校准随机性，从而在保证隐私的同时减少传统差分隐私方法带来的噪声和训练偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO训练样本中的提示词和候选回答通常可公开或由模型生成，但“哪一个回答更受偏好”的标签来自主观判断，可能泄露标注者或用户的敏感信息。通用隐私保护训练方法未利用DPO数据结构，往往需要对完整的逐样本梯度进行裁剪和加噪，导致噪声过大、计算成本高，并可能造成有偏更新。因此，论文旨在设计一种仅保护偏好标签、且适配大规模LLM训练的隐私机制。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为“偏好隐私”，即一种类似标签差分隐私的定义：攻击者已知提示词及两个候选回答，仅需隐藏二者之间的偏好信号。论文观察到，两个仅在偏好标签上不同的相邻样本，其DPO梯度差异始终位于由文本内容决定的一维“偏好轴”上，因此偏好信息只经由该方向传递。PrivDPO通过对DPO目标进行无偏随机重缩放，仅沿该偏好轴加入经校准的随机性，而不需要传统DP-SGD式的逐样本梯度计算、裁剪和全空间加噪。

Result: 在三个对齐基准和三个LLM模型家族上的实验表明，PrivDPO相较隐私保护基线方法能稳定取得更优的隐私—效用折中。摘要强调，该方法既能有效保护相对偏好标签，又避免了对与偏好无关参数方向施加噪声，因此能够保留更好的对齐性能，并具备大规模训练兼容性。

Conclusion: 该工作针对DPO中的核心敏感对象——相对偏好标签——提出了结构化、任务专用的隐私保护框架。其关键贡献在于识别并利用偏好信息在梯度空间中的一维结构，以低开销、无偏的随机重缩放替代通用方法的全梯度扰动。论文结论是：针对数据与优化结构定制隐私机制，可显著改善LLM偏好对齐中的隐私保护与模型效用平衡。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization (DPO) is now a standard method for aligning large language models (LLMs) using human preference data. Each DPO example contains a prompt and a pair of candidate model responses. While prompts and responses are often public or model-generated, the relative preference between responses reflects subjective judgments and can reveal sensitive attributes of annotators or end users. Off-the-shelf privacy-preserving approaches are not well matched to this structure, leading to unnecessary noise injection and biased updates in training.
  In this paper, we formalize preference privacy, a label-DP-style privacy notion for DPO that protects only the relative preference between candidate responses, assuming an adversary who already knows the prompt and responses. We then design PrivDPO, a DPO variant that enforces preference privacy while remaining compatible with large-scale LLM training. Our main observation is that, for neighboring examples differing only in their preference signal, the gradient difference lies on a one-dimensional preference axis determined solely by the text; all preference information flows through this axis. PrivDPO adds calibrated randomness only along this axis via an unbiased randomized rescaling of the DPO objective, avoiding per-example gradient operations. Our experiments on three alignment benchmarks and three LLM families show that PrivDPO consistently achieves strong privacy-utility trade-offs compared with privacy-preserving baselines.

</details>


### [109] [Gradient Immunity: Null-Space Resistance to Malicious Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05045)
*Yuxuan Huang,Xingyu Zeng,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向部分保护开放权重发布（PPOW）的Unidirectional Safety Gate（USG），通过在模型末层后插入受保护的安全组件，限制有害数据在下游微调中的梯度更新，从而提高恶意微调的难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御多针对微调即服务场景，或依赖下游使用者额外遵循安全流程，难以适用于开放权重但保留少量不可训练安全组件的发布模式。论文旨在在不要求下游合作的前提下，维持模型发布后的安全对齐能力。

Method: USG由Null Space Cubic Layer和Inverse Adapter组成，并置于最终Transformer层之后。前者根据防御方持有的有害样本校准表示空间中的保护区域及阈值；微调时，落入该区域的有害样本其梯度会被抑制或阻断。后者用于补偿前向传播变化，尽量恢复基础模型原有行为。

Result: 在六组模型—数据集实验中，USG在固定发布阈值下使微调后的攻击成功率维持在接近发布前的水平；在较容易的设置中仍保持较高的安全通过率。针对BeaverTails中的不安全样本，方法呈现更清晰的安全性—效用权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，发布阶段基于表示空间的梯度阻断能够在保留大部分可训练权重的同时，降低下游恶意适配的有效性。其主要贡献是提供了一种无需依赖下游用户配合的开放权重安全发布机制，但保护范围依赖于校准数据与表示分布的覆盖程度。

Abstract: Released aligned large language models remain vulnerable to malicious downstream finetuning. Existing defenses are largely designed for the fine-tuning-as-a-service (FTaaS) paradigm or rely on downstream users to follow additional safety procedures, and therefore do not directly address the setting we study: a provider controlled partially protected open-weight (PPOW) release setting in which most weights remain trainable while a small safety-critical component is preserved at release. We propose a Unidirectional Safety Gate (USG), instantiated as a Null Space Cubic Layer together with an Inverse Adapter inserted after the final Transformer layer. During downstream fine-tuning, the cubic layer suppresses or blocks gradients from harmful samples whose hidden states fall in a calibrated protected region, while the Inverse Adapter restores the base model's forward behavior. In practice, we calibrate a threshold using defender-held harmful data, allowing protection to generalize to nearby in-distribution harmful samples. Across six evaluated model-dataset settings, USG keeps post-finetuning attack success rate close to the pre-release level under a fixed release threshold, while maintaining high safe-pass rates on easier settings and exhibiting a clearer safety-utility trade-off on unsafe samples from BeaverTails. These results suggest that release-time representation-space blocking can raise the cost of malicious downstream adaptation without requiring downstream cooperation. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/Gradient-Immunity.

</details>


### [110] [Kerckhoffs-Compliant Watermarking for Physical Design IP Protection: From Placement to Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05055)
*Andrew B. Kahng,Yiting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PDMarks，一种仅依赖秘密密钥、符合Kerckhoffs原则的多阶段物理设计水印框架，可在布局、时钟树综合和布线阶段嵌入并验证IP所有权证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理设计水印方法通常只覆盖单一实现阶段，或依赖不公开的构造细节，因此难以抵抗白盒攻击。随着物理设计工具的普及，已完成布局布线数据库的未授权复用风险上升，需要一种跨阶段、可公开算法且安全性仅依赖密钥的保护机制。

Method: PDMarks使用32字节秘密密钥和HMAC-SHA256，确定性地生成水印实例及其目标值，并将水印嵌入布局、时钟树综合和布线等多个阶段。该框架已集成到OpenROAD-flow-scripts中，通过联合验证各阶段的所有权证据来完成认证，并采用错误密钥和攻击实验评估安全性。

Result: 在NanGate45和ASAP7设计上的实验表明，PDMarks相比已有物理设计水印方法能够提供更强的所有权证据，同时保持相当或更低的功耗、性能和面积开销。所有评测设计的多阶段联合偶合概率均低于10^-32；错误密钥无法重现完整所有权证明，削弱水印则需要对受保护实现进行大范围扰动。

Conclusion: PDMarks实现了跨布局、CTS和布线阶段的统一物理设计IP保护，并在较低PPA开销下显著提升了证据强度和抗攻击能力。结果支持其作为实际VLSI流程中可部署的、密钥驱动的物理设计水印方案。

Abstract: Physical design (PD) intellectual property (IP) is a valuable artifact of modern VLSI implementation. It includes optimized cell placement, clock distribution, and routing decisions produced by carefully tuned PD flows. As access to PD tools expands, unauthorized reuse of placed-and-routed databases becomes an increasing concern. Existing PD watermarking methods either protect only one PD stage or rely on hidden construction details, leaving them vulnerable to a white-box adversary. In this work, we develop PDMarks, a Kerckhoffs-compliant watermarking framework whose security depends only on a secret key. PDMarks embeds ownership evidence across multiple stages of the PD flow, including placement, clock tree synthesis (CTS), and routing. All watermark instances and target values are deterministically derived from a 32-byte secret key using HMAC-SHA256, enabling consistent embedding and verification. PDMarks has been integrated into OpenROAD-flow-scripts. Experiments on NanGate45 and ASAP7 designs show that PDMarks outperforms prior physical design watermarking methods by providing much stronger ownership evidence with comparable or smaller PPA overhead. The approximate joint all-stage coincidence probability is below 10^{-32} for every evaluated design. Wrong-key and attack evaluations further show that incorrect keys do not reproduce the complete ownership proof and that weakening the watermark requires broad perturbation of the protected implementation.

</details>


### [111] [Hardware Design and Security in the Era of Chiplets and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05063)
*Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Paul V. Gratz,Ramesh Karri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The semiconductor industry is undergoing a dual revolution: the shift toward heterogeneous 2.5D chiplet systems and the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Electronic Design Automation (EDA) flows. While these paradigms offer unprecedented benefits in yield, modularity, design productivity, etc., they radically expand the hardware attack surface. This paper provides a unified analysis of these frontiers, ranging from attacks on chiplet systems (including hardware stacks for LLM acceleration) across architectural, logical, and physical levels, to various exploits against LLM-driven EDA pipelines. To secure chiplet systems, we review a powerful defense approach that leverages 2.5D split manufacturing and active interposers for physically isolated Root of Trust (RoT) architectures. To secure LLM-driven EDA pipelines, we first identify native threats and then review state-of-the-art defense techniques. Finally, we discuss how LLM systems can advance hardware security efforts for modern systems, including chiplets.

</details>


### [112] [Agent Against Agent: An Agentic System for Automatic Prompt Injection Red Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05108)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Runpeng Geng,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIMiner，一种面向大语言模型智能体的提示注入自动化红队测试系统。它通过构建可迁移的策略库，在无需针对新目标模型额外训练的情况下，仅使用少量查询即可实现较高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入红队测试方法主要依赖强化学习，训练得到的攻击者模型往往难以泛化到未见过的目标大语言模型。因此，需要一种查询效率高、跨模型迁移能力强且可用于安全评估与训练数据收集的方法。

Method: PIMiner在训练阶段依次处理多个“数据集—目标模型”组合，并从零构建提示注入策略库；测试阶段将该策略库直接迁移至未见过的目标模型，无需额外训练。每个测试样本仅向目标智能体发起少量查询，例如10次。

Result: 在IPIArena上，PIMiner对Gemini-2.5-Pro、GPT-5.1和Claude-Sonnet-4.5的攻击成功率分别为76.2%、61.9%和42.9%；在AgentDojo上分别达到86.7%、53.3%和40.0%。结果表明，该方法在不同目标模型和基准数据集上均具备较强性能。

Conclusion: PIMiner通过策略库实现了提示注入攻击策略的跨目标模型迁移，并在低查询预算下取得较高攻击成功率，显示出其在大语言模型智能体安全评估和防御训练数据生成中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Prompt injection poses significant security risks to LLM agents. Efficient and effective red-teaming is therefore critical, both for evaluating these risks and for collecting training data to improve defenses. Existing state-of-the-art prompt injection red-teaming methods primarily rely on reinforcement learning (RL), producing attacker models that often generalize poorly to new target LLMs. In this work, we develop PIMiner, an agentic system for prompt injection red-teaming. During training, PIMiner is trained on a sequence of (dataset, target model) pairs and builds a strategy library from scratch. At test time, the learned strategy library can be directly transferred to a previously unseen target LLM without additional training. PIMiner requires only a small number of queries to a target agent (e.g., 10) per test sample. Experimental results demonstrate that PIMiner achieves strong performance. On IPIArena, it attains a 76.2% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 61.9% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 42.9% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5. On AgentDojo, it achieves an 86.7% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 53.3% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 40.0% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5.

</details>


### [113] [Temporal Context Awareness: A Defense Framework Against Multi-turn Manipulation Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15560)
*Prashant Kulkarni,Assaf Namer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly vulnerable to sophisticated multi-turn manipulation attacks, where adversaries strategically build context through seemingly benign conversational turns to circumvent safety measures and elicit harmful or unauthorized responses. These attacks exploit the temporal nature of dialogue to evade single-turn detection methods, representing a critical security vulnerability with significant implications for real-world deployments.
  This paper introduces the Temporal Context Awareness (TCA) framework, a novel defense mechanism designed to address this challenge by continuously analyzing semantic drift, cross-turn intention consistency and evolving conversational patterns. The TCA framework integrates dynamic context embedding analysis, cross-turn consistency verification, and progressive risk scoring to detect and mitigate manipulation attempts effectively. Preliminary evaluations on simulated adversarial scenarios demonstrate the framework's potential to identify subtle manipulation patterns often missed by traditional detection techniques, offering a much-needed layer of security for conversational AI systems. In addition to outlining the design of TCA , we analyze diverse attack vectors and their progression across multi-turn conversation, providing valuable insights into adversarial tactics and their impact on LLM vulnerabilities. Our findings underscore the pressing need for robust, context-aware defenses in conversational AI systems and highlight TCA framework as a promising direction for securing LLMs while preserving their utility in legitimate applications. We make our implementation available to support further research in this emerging area of AI security.

</details>
